Read it as JSON in python (`json.load`), look up each name, read its `type` field. DO NOT grep for Lean names.\n- This section is the one where a name most flatters its content. `noHairTheorem_statement` is named \"statement\" for a reason; read it and describe what it asserts, not what the no-hair theorem asserts in the literature.\n- Where a theorem is a definitional unfolding rather than a derivation, say which.\n- Cite as `\\texttt{ModuleName.theorem\\_name}` (escape underscores).\n\n## PROSE LAW\n- NO em-dash. Never `---`, never a literal em-dash character. `--` (exactly two hyphens) is MANDATORY for compound eponyms (`Hawking--Penrose`, `Levi-Civita` is a single hyphen and stays as it is, `Bianchi`, `Kerr--Newman` if used) and numeric ranges and must never be removed. {"parentUuid":"6546a25c-5737-4ea7-b630-74f4335b9327","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a1b16a56a1a83bc81","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NyvB4iWbw4Yhr4cHfS6AYE","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Prohibited patterns in manuscript prose\n2\t\n3\t**Shared reference. Read on drafting, and read again on review.** The `paper-authoring`\n4\tskill and the `prose-reviewer` agent both consume this file, so a rule cannot mean one\n5\tthing while writing and another while reviewing.\n6\t\n7\t⚠️ **This is the shared FLOOR, not the whole review.** The reviewer additionally carries\n8\treader-outcome questions that do not appear here and that an author must never be given as\n9\ta checklist: a generator optimises against any list it can see. See the skill's §\"Why the\n10\treviewer knows things this file does not\".\n11\t\n12\tEvery rule below was derived from a measured sweep of the 21-bundle corpus, not from a\n13\tstyle guide. Where a number appears, it is what was counted.\n14\t\n15\t---\n16\t\n17\t## 1. The em-dash: prohibited outright, including in headings\n18\t\n19\t**Never write `---` or a literal `—`.** Two forms exist and both are banned.\n20\t\n21\t**Why this one is absolute.** An em-dash reads as machine-authored to a 2026 reader and\n22\tcosts trust before the physics is evaluated. One is as disqualifying as forty, so there is\n23\tno acceptable density. Enforced by `validate.py --check bundle_prose_em_dash_free`, target\n24\tzero. Measured before the corpus was swept: **741 of them, and 0 of 21 bundles clean.**\n25\t\n26\t### ⚠️ The en-dash `--` is MANDATORY and must never be touched\n27\t\n28\t`--` (exactly two hyphens) is a different character doing a different job, and the corpus\n29\tcarries **1,121** of them:\n30\t\n31\t- compound eponyms: `Bose--Einstein`, `Bekenstein--Hawking`, `Schwinger--Keldysh`,\n32\t  `Kaul--Majumdar`, `Chowdhury--Hartnoll--Hebbar--Khondaker`, `SK--EFT`\n33\t- numeric ranges: `pp. 10--15`, `$2$--$3$ K`\n34\t\n35\t**An author over-correcting to \"no dashes at all\" breaks every compound name in the\n36\tprogram, and the check cannot see it** because it counts exactly three hyphens. This is the\n37\tsingle highest-cost mistake available here. One live line contained both:\n38\t`observables---also drives the SK--EFT`.\n39\t\n40\tA third variant exists in the wild: the literal Unicode en-dash `–` (U+2013), found as\n41\t`Kaul–Majumdar` and `Chowdhury–Hartnoll–Hebbar–Khondaker`. Write `--` instead; D3 once\n42\tspelled the same name both ways.\n43\t\n44\t### Replacing an em-dash is a REWRITE, not a substitution\n45\t\n46\tA mechanical swap produces comma splices. Identify the job the dash was doing, then use the\n47\tpunctuation that already exists for that job. Frequencies below are from six independent\n48\treadings of all 741 occurrences.\n49\t\n50\t| The dash was doing | Frequency | Write instead |\n51\t|---|---|---|\n52\t| Paired aside interrupting a clause | ~45–55% | parentheses; a comma pair when the aside is short and contains no commas of its own |\n53\t| Introducing an explanation, expansion or list | ~30–45% | a colon |\n54\t| Separating a label from its gloss in `\\item`, `\\subsection{}`, `\\paragraph{}` | ~15% | a colon, always |\n55\t| Contrastive \"X, not Y\" interjection | ~7% | a comma pair (parentheses would demote a correction to an aside) |\n56\t| Joining two independent clauses | ~3–15% | a semicolon, or a full stop and a new sentence |\n57\t| Trailing appositive glossing a number's significance | common in E1/E2 | a comma |\n58\t\n59\t### The four hard cases, each of which defeats a mechanical fix\n60\t\n61\t1. **The aside contains its own parenthetical or citation.** Converting the outer dash to\n62\t   parentheses yields stacked or nested parens that read as a stutter:\n63\t   `(\\lean{diamondDist_eq_choiSDP}) (the Watrous semidefinite program~\\cite{Watrous2018}) is proven`.\n64\t   **Merge the tag and the aside into ONE parenthetical** with an internal comma or\n65\t   semicolon. Do not nest.\n66\t2. **The aside contains a comma-separated list.** Comma-punctuating it makes the reader\n67\t   parse the inner items as continuing the outer sentence's list. Use parentheses.\n68\t3. **The sentence already has a colon later on.** Adding a second colon reads as a nested\n69\t   label. Restructure with a copula: `X is the headline theorem: Y`.\n70\t4. **Three levels of subordination.** An aside inside an aside inside a main clause is a\n71\t   sentence trying to be three sentences. Split it.\n72\t\n73\t### The generative habits behind them: prohibit these, not just the symbol\n74\t\n75\tAll six readers converged on the same root, so treat it as the rule that matters:\n76\t\n77\t> **Do not interrupt a main clause to insert a clarification.** Write the clarification as\n78\t> its own sentence, before or after.\n79\t\n80\tThe dominant shape was *claim, dash, restatement-with-a-twist, dash, continuation*: draft a\n81\tclean declarative, then, worried a reader will misattribute a convention or miss a caveat,\n82\tsplice the qualifier into the middle. It concentrated in exactly the paragraphs doing the\n83\theaviest epistemic hedging (novelty disclaimers, provenance caveats, convention-pinning),\n84\twhich is where this project's prose is densest.\n85\t\n86\tFour more shapes, each of which reliably produces an em-dash:\n87\t\n88\t- **Name-then-gloss in itemised lists.** `\\item \\verb|foo_bar|: the substantive witness`.\n89\t  Use the colon from the start; this was the single most common pattern in the Lean-heavy\n90\t  bundles.\n91\t- **Label-then-gloss in headings.** `\\subsection{Pillar 1: Golterman--Shamir}`. A dash in a\n92\t  heading also reads as a tic when scanning the table of contents.\n93\t- **Tag-stacking.** A claim ending in a `\\lean{...}` identifier in parens, then a dash\n94\t  glossing that identifier. This is an appositive that got dash-punctuated only because the\n95\t  sentence had already spent its parentheses.\n96\t- **Claim-then-immediately-re-scope.** `T_H \\approx 2.4$ K --- nine orders of magnitude\n97\t  above atomic BEC --- with adiabaticity...`. A defensible instinct for a rigorous program,\n98\t  executed with the wrong punctuation: pick the syntax that matches the qualifier's\n99\t  grammatical role.\n100\t\n101\t---\n102\t\n103\t## 2. A fix may not narrate itself\n104\t\n105\t**Never tell the reader what an earlier draft of this paper said, when it was corrected, or\n106\twhich review round caught it.** A referee has no access to that process and cannot act on\n107\tit; the text reads as a changelog pasted into a manuscript.\n108\t\n109\tEnforced by `validate.py --check bundle_reader_facing_voice`, target zero. Banned forms:\n110\t\n111\t- a correction stamped with a date: `(corrected 2026-08-01)`\n112\t- an account of an earlier draft: `three earlier drafts of this paragraph asserted...`\n113\t- a first-person superseded claim: `We previously shipped a theorem asserting...`\n114\t- an internal review reference: `(D11 Stage-13 round-14 finding 6.2)`\n115\t- the project's own planning documents: `Our earlier planning documents described...`\n116\t- review rounds cast as actors: `rounds 7 and 10 both rated this cosmetic`\n117\t- a claim about the paper's own drafting: `typeset unconditionally in every draft`\n118\t\n119\t**The history is not lost, it moves.** `papers/<X>/change_log.md` and\n120\t`docs/review_finding_supersessions.json` are where a later reader can actually check it, and\n121\tthe ledger entry is required by the lift procedure anyway.\n122\t\n123\t### ⚠️ Removing narration is not deleting content\n124\t\n125\tMost of these passages wrap something real, and the substance must survive:\n126\t\n127\t- **A retraction is a scientific disclosure.** Restate it as a present-tense negative\n128\t  result: what is *not* claimed and why it fails. Do not delete the paragraph.\n129\t- **A scope correction states the correct scope.** Keep every factual statement about what\n130\t  a module does and does not contain, including file:line references. Drop only \"earlier\n131\t  drafts called it X\" and the date/round/finding tag.\n132\t- **A completeness disclaimer is a real caveat.** Keep it; drop the account of what two\n133\t  earlier drafts asserted.\n134\t\n135\t### What is NOT prohibited\n136\t\n137\tReporting that *the literature* corrected something is ordinary scholarship: `Smith\n138\tcorrected this coefficient in 2019` is correct and stays.\n139\t\n140\tDescribing the verification pipeline is legitimate **when the pipeline is the paper's\n141\tsubject matter** (I1, and I2/I3 for library conventions). Addressing the paper's own\n142\treferees is legitimate anywhere: `lets Mathlib4 reviewers calibrate expectations`.\n143\t\n144\tThis distinction is why the rule targets the *act* of self-narration rather than a\n145\tvocabulary. A word list banning `Stage 13`, `reviewer` and `adversarial review` scores 90\n146\thits on this corpus, of which 48 are legitimate uses in the methodology paper alone.\n147\t\n148\t---\n149\t\n150\t## 3. Claim discipline\n151\t\n152\tThese are enforced elsewhere in the pipeline; they are repeated here because they are\n153\tcheaper to satisfy while drafting than to repair at review.\n154\t\n155\t- **Never hedge a count or numerical literal with \"alt convention\" or \"+N depending on\n156\t  convention\" without proving the convention sensitivity.** If counts disagree across\n157\t  tools, find the truth. Convention-drift framing masks actual content drift: a \"161 ± 3\n158\t  alt convention\" was really 164, and the +3 was real drift.\n159\t- **Verify which registry a name lives in before citing it.** `AXIOM_METADATA`,\n160\t  `PLACEHOLDER_THEOREMS`, `HYPOTHESIS_REGISTRY` and `BORDISM_HYPOTHESES` are four different\n161\t  tables; a name found in one and attributed to another is a Stage-13 blocker.\n162\t- **Every cross-language prior-art claim must resolve to a verifiable URL or canonical\n163\t  ecosystem reference.** Hallucinated libraries have shipped before. When in doubt, cite\n164\t  the ecosystem (UniMath, Mathlib4) rather than naming a specific library.\n165\t- **When broadening an attribution, verify every addition belongs in the broadened\n166\t  category.** Surface code is abelian-stabiliser QEC, not non-Abelian-anyon TQC.\n167\t- **Numbers come from the pipeline.** Use `\\input{tables/<spec>.tex}` and the `counts.tex`\n168\t  macros rather than typing a literal.\n169\t\n170\t---\n171\t\n172\t## 4. Structure\n173\t\n174\t- **The abstract leads with the result**, not with the programme, the architecture, or what\n175\t  the paper will do.\n176\t- **The reader never sees the project's internal taxonomy.** Not `bundle`, `tier`, `wave`,\n177\t  `phase`, or `splash`. Companion papers are cited as papers, by title, in a normal\n178\t  reference list. This is what makes a manuscript read as a repository index: F's organising\n179\t  vocabulary was its own publication plan.\n180\t- **Section architecture is authored, not accumulated.** A bundle's sections come from its\n181\t  charter, written before any source is registered. Do not add a section because a source\n182\t  was registered: D3 has 37 sections against 31 sources, which is sedimentation.\n183\t- **Every figure is planned.** A bundle with no figures is a charter defect, not a style\n184\t  choice; 9 of 21 bundles shipped zero figures because the lift step only ever *migrated*\n185\t  figures that already existed.\n186\t"}]},"uuid":"c64a1376-0cf4-4f58-b9b6-b07136e57b1b","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:39:28.437Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6546a25c-5737-4ea7-b630-74f4335b9327","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}

{"parentUuid":"efb7b75e-4ac0-44bc-abd4-09549e7c3e69","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a1b16a56a1a83bc81","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EvU9dt9yU3WB4rkfwyVn2e","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: paper-authoring\n3\tdescription: This skill should be used when the user asks to \"draft a paper section\", \"write the bundle draft\", \"author the manuscript\", \"lift content into a bundle\", \"write up this wave for publication\", \"draft the abstract\", \"revise the paper prose\", or when editing any `papers/<bundle>/paper_draft.tex`. Provides the house voice, the prohibited-pattern floor, and venue conventions for SK-EFT Hawking publication bundles.\n4\t---\n5\t\n6\t# Authoring a publication bundle\n7\t\n8\tGuidance for writing manuscript prose in `papers/<bundle>/paper_draft.tex`. This is the\n9\tgenerative counterpart to the `prose-reviewer` agent; both read the same prohibited-pattern\n10\treference so a rule cannot drift between writing and review.\n11\t\n12\tRead it either way you draft: the lead writing in-context, or a `paper-drafter` agent\n13\tdispatched over one section. `paper-drafter` reads this file and the same reference by\n14\tpath, so the floor is identical however the prose is produced.\n15\t\n16\t**Read `references/prohibited-patterns.md` before drafting.** It is mandatory, not\n17\tbackground, and it is short.\n18\t\n19\t---\n20\t\n21\t## Why this skill exists\n22\t\n23\tThe pipeline had three reviewer agents and no authoring guidance. Everything about how to\n24\twrite lived in `docs/BUNDLE_LIFT_PROCEDURE.md` §7 as a bookkeeping checklist consulted at\n25\tlift time. The measured result of that gap, across 21 bundles:\n26\t\n27\t- **741 em-dashes**, with zero bundles clean\n28\t- **13 passages** narrating the paper's own correction history to a referee\n29\t- **9 of 21 bundles shipping zero figures**, because the lift step only migrated figures\n30\t  that already existed and no step ever planned one\n31\t- **D3 carrying 37 sections against 31 contributing sources**, which is sedimentation\n32\t  rather than architecture\n33\t\n34\tNone of that is a capability limit. It is what an unguided generator produces when the only\n35\tcontrols are downstream reviewers.\n36\t\n37\t---\n38\t\n39\t## Before writing a section\n40\t\n41\t1. **Read the charter.** `papers/<X>/bundle_metadata.json` carries `target_journal` and\n42\t   `length_target`; the section belongs to a planned outline, not to a source paper that\n43\t   happened to be registered.\n44\t2. **Read the substrate directly.** Contributing per-paper drafts, the Lean modules, the\n45\t   roadmap. Do not write from a summary.\n46\t3. **Know the claim this section makes** and which apex theorem backs it. A section whose\n47\t   claim has no Lean witness is a finding waiting to happen.\n48\t\n49\t## While writing\n50\t\n51\tWrite for a referee at the named venue who has never seen the repository, cannot run the\n52\tcode, and will not read a second sentence to understand the first.\n53\t\n54\t- **State the result, then support it.** Not the programme, not the architecture, not what\n55\t  the section will do.\n56\t- **One clause, one job.** Do not interrupt a main clause to insert a clarification; write\n57\t  the clarification as its own sentence. This single habit produced most of the corpus's\n58\t  em-dashes.\n59\t- **No em-dash, ever** (`---` or `—`). **`--` is mandatory** for compound eponyms and\n60\t  ranges and must never be touched. Both rules and every replacement case are in\n61\t  `references/prohibited-patterns.md` §1; read them before reaching for a dash.\n62\t- **A fix never narrates itself.** Correct silently in the manuscript and record the history\n63\t  in `change_log.md` and the supersession ledger (§2 of the reference).\n64\t- **Numbers come from the pipeline** via `\\input{tables/<spec>.tex}` and the `counts.tex`\n65\t  macros, never typed as literals.\n66\t- **Cite companion work as papers**, by title. The reader never sees `bundle`, `tier`,\n67\t  `wave`, `phase` or `splash`.\n68\t\n69\t## Before handing off\n70\t\n71\t```bash\n72\tuv run python scripts/compile_bundle_pdf.py <X>   # must be clean\n73\tuv run python scripts/validate.py \\\n74\t    --check bundle_prose_em_dash_free \\\n75\t    --check bundle_reader_facing_voice \\\n76\t    --check bundle_manuscript_length\n77\t```\n78\t\n79\tCompile through `compile_bundle_pdf.py`, never `pdflatex` inside `papers/<X>/`. An in-place\n80\tcompile clobbers `paper_draft.{aux,log,synctex.gz}`, and a reviewer running concurrently then\n81\tjudges your draft by a `.log` that belongs to someone else's pass.\n82\t\n83\tAll three are deterministic and all three must pass. They are a floor, not a review: they\n84\tconfirm nothing prohibited is present, and say nothing about whether the argument carries.\n85\t\n86\t---\n87\t\n88\t## Why the reviewer knows things this file does not\n89\t\n90\tThe `prose-reviewer` agent reads this same reference, so the two never disagree about a\n91\trule. It also carries a brief that is deliberately **not** written down here, framed on\n92\treader outcome rather than rule compliance: where a reader would stop, whether the abstract\n93\tleads with the result, whether each section advances a single argument.\n94\t\n95\tThat asymmetry is intentional. A generator optimises against any checklist it can see, so a\n96\treviewer briefed entirely off the author's rules would pass anything the author produced.\n97\tSatisfying this file is necessary and not sufficient.\n98\t\n99\t---\n100\t\n101\t## Additional resources\n102\t\n103\t### Reference files\n104\t\n105\t- **`references/prohibited-patterns.md`** — mandatory read. The em-dash prohibition and its\n106\t  replacement cases, the self-narration ban, claim discipline, structure rules. Shared with\n107\t  the `prose-reviewer` agent.\n108\t\n109\t### Related surfaces\n110\t\n111\t- `docs/BUNDLE_LIFT_PROCEDURE.md` — the 14-step lift workflow this drafting sits inside\n112\t  (§7 authoring, §11 the BLOCKER loop, §12a the terminal de-scarring pass).\n113\t- `docs/WAVE_EXECUTION_PIPELINE.md` Stage 10 — the process law and its gates.\n114\t- `docs/BUNDLE_DIRECTORY_SCHEMA.md` — `length_target`, `apex_theorems`, and who writes what.\n115\t"}]},"uuid":"1cd50d59-9caf-4dcd-aa9b-d7633517fbcc","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:39:37.989Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"efb7b75e-4ac0-44bc-abd4-09549e7c3e69","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}
{"parentUuid":"72eb3bc9-6f36-417b-a9a1-60b02145947c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a1b16a56a1a83bc81","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011fmJBzM3d4cSZ7Kmy8nzXZ","type":"tool_result","content":"1900\t\\texttt{perturbed\\_alpha\\_not\\_H\\_EinsteinCartanExtensionHolds}).\n1901\t\n1902\t\n1903\t%% =====================================================================\n1904\t%% §22.5 — Algebraic-GR backbone (paper44 + Phase 6f-6g)\n1905\t%% =====================================================================\n1906\t\\section{Algebraic Lorentzian-geometry backbone (substrate appendix)}\n1907\t\\label{sec:algebraic-gr-backbone}\n1908\t\n1909\tThe classical-gravity backbone supporting the singularity, area, and no-hair\n1910\tcontent of \\S\\ref{sec:causal-structure}--\\S\\ref{sec:no-hair} requires\n1911\talgebraic Lorentzian-geometry primitives that are not part of\n1912\tMathlib4's standard Riemannian-geometry library (which assumes\n1913\tpositive-definite metrics).\n1914\tThe supplement in \\texttt{LorentzianMetric.lean},\n1915\t\\texttt{RiemannianConnection.lean}, \\texttt{RiemannCoordinate.lean},\n1916\t\\texttt{RiemannDifferentialBianchi.lean},\n1917\t\\texttt{BundleRiemann.lean}, and \\texttt{BundleRiemannAux.lean} fills this\n1918\tgap. The bundle is documented in I1 (methodology) at primary scope;\n1919\t\\S\\ref{sec:algebraic-gr-backbone} is the substrate-side supplement\n1920\tconsuming it.\n1921\t\n1922\t\\subsection{Lorentzian metric typeclass}\n1923\t\n1924\tThe Lorentzian-metric typeclass declares an indefinite-signature inner\n1925\tproduct on a finite-dimensional real vector space, with the\n1926\t$(-,+,+,+)$ signature as the canonical instance. The typeclass admits a\n1927\tbundle structure that supports the classical singularity-theorem and\n1928\tno-hair content of subsequent sections.\n1929\t\n1930\t\\subsection{Levi-Civita Christoffel uniqueness on Lorentzian metrics}\n1931\t\n1932\tThe Christoffel-symbol Levi-Civita uniqueness theorem, which on\n1933\tRiemannian manifolds is well known and Mathlib-shipped, was, to our\n1934\tknowledge, not previously formalised on Lorentzian\n1935\t(indefinite-signature) metrics in any proof assistant.\n1936\t\\texttt{RiemannianConnection.lean} discharges\n1937\tuniqueness on the indefinite-signature case; this is the\n1938\tload-bearing first-formalization claim of\n1939\t\\S\\ref{sec:algebraic-gr-backbone}.\n1940\t\n1941\t\\subsection{Algebraic first Bianchi identity}\n1942\t\n1943\tThe first Bianchi identity (algebraic symmetry of the Riemann tensor's\n1944\tcyclic-three-index sum) closes by\n1945\t\\texttt{BundleRiemann.bundleRiemannAux\\_first\\_bianchi\\_torsionfree\\_apply}\n1946\t(with the connection-level companion\n1947\t\\texttt{bundleRiemannAux\\_first\\_bianchi\\_torsionfree\\_of\\_isCovariantDerivativeOn});\n1948\tthe differential second Bianchi identity, which on a torsion-free\n1949\tconnection states $\\nabla_{[\\mu} R_{\\nu\\rho]\\sigma\\tau} = 0$, is captured\n1950\tthrough the antisymmetry-supporting machinery of\n1951\t\\texttt{RiemannDifferentialBianchi.lean} (theorems\n1952\t\\texttt{covRiemann\\_AntisymLastTwo} and\n1953\t\\texttt{covRiemann\\_coord\\_AntisymLastTwo} in the in-coordinate\n1954\tformulation, with the contracted second-Bianchi identity supplied by\n1955\t\\texttt{DifferentialSecondBianchi}).\n1956\t\n1957\t\\subsection{Algebraic Riemann tensor + Einstein tensor}\n1958\t\n1959\tThe Riemann tensor on the Lorentzian-metric bundle (\\texttt{Curvature.lean},\n1960\t\\texttt{EinsteinTensor.lean}) and the Einstein tensor with its trace-reverse\n1961\tidentity carry over from the supplement infrastructure. The exact-solution\n1962\tmachinery (\\texttt{ExactSolutions.lean}, \\texttt{KerrSchild.lean}) closes\n1963\tthe Schwarzschild Kerr--Schild form and the de~Sitter / Anti-de~Sitter\n1964\t$\\Lambda$-vacuum biconditional.\n1965\t\n1966\t\\subsection{Energy conditions on the bundle}\n1967\t\n1968\t\\texttt{EnergyConditions.lean} encodes the four energy conditions\n1969\t(NEC, WEC, SEC, DEC) on the Lorentzian-metric bundle as predicates over\n1970\tstress-energy. These predicates are consumed by the\n1971\tPenrose-hypothesis bundle of \\S\\ref{sec:penrose} and the area theorem of\n1972\t\\S\\ref{sec:area}.\n1973\t\n1974\t\\subsection{ADM Hamiltonian + momentum constraint}\n1975\t\n1976\t\\texttt{ADMFormalism.lean} encodes the Arnowitt--Deser--Misner Hamiltonian\n1977\tformulation of general relativity~\\cite{ADM1962}, with the momentum\n1978\tconstraint and Hamiltonian constraint as Lean biconditionals over the\n1979\tindefinite-signature metric bundle. This is required infrastructure for\n1980\tthe regime-partition of \\S\\ref{sec:bch-four-laws} and the no-hair theorem\n1981\tof \\S\\ref{sec:no-hair}.\n1982\t\n1983\t\\subsection{Tetrad-metric equivalence}\n1984\t\n1985\tThe substrate's tetrad fields (Akama--Diakonov--Wetterich apparatus) and\n1986\tthe Lorentzian metric on the same bundle satisfy a Lean equivalence\n1987\trelation: the metric is the tetrad's bilinear $g_{\\mu\\nu} = \\eta_{ab}\\,e^a_\\mu\\,e^b_\\nu$,\n1988\tand the tetrad is recoverable from the metric up to local Lorentz\n1989\ttransformation. This equivalence is the load-bearing infrastructure for\n1990\tthe substrate's tetrad-condensation phase to inherit the classical-GR\n1991\tcontent of subsequent sections.\n1992\t\n1993\t\n1994\t%% =====================================================================\n1995\t%% §23 — Causal structure axioms\n1996\t%% =====================================================================\n1997\t\\subsection{Causal structure axioms}\n1998\t\\label{sec:causal-structure}\n1999\t\n2000\tThe substrate's spacetime carries the causal structure axioms of\n2001\tWald~\\cite{Wald1984} \\S 8.1.\n2002\t\n2003\t\\subsection{Causal structure axiom hierarchy}\n2004\t\n2005\tFollowing Wald \\S 8.1, we declare the predicate hierarchy:\n2006\t\\emph{chronology} (no closed timelike curve),\n2007\t\\emph{causality} (no closed causal curve),\n2008\t\\emph{strong causality} (small causal-violation neighbourhoods),\n2009\t\\emph{stable causality} (small Lorentzian perturbations preserve causality).\n2010\tEach predicate is a Lean Prop on the spacetime bundle of \\S\\ref{sec:algebraic-gr-backbone};\n2011\tthe hierarchy\n2012\t$\\text{stable} \\Rightarrow \\text{strong} \\Rightarrow \\text{causality} \\Rightarrow \\text{chronology}$\n2013\tcloses constructively.\n2014\t\n2015\t\\subsection{The realLineSpacetime witness}\n2016\t\n2017\t\\texttt{realLineSpacetime} is the substrate's concrete witness instance:\n2018\ta $1+0$-dimensional flat spacetime that satisfies all four causal-structure\n2019\tpredicates trivially. The witness exists for the substrate at the\n2020\tmachine-checked level\n2021\t(\\texttt{realLineSpacetime\\_no\\_trappedSurface\\_for\\_nonneg\\_expansion}).\n2022\t\n2023\t\\subsection{Bundle to substrate}\n2024\t\n2025\t\\S\\ref{sec:causal-structure}'s causal-structure hierarchy is consumed by\n2026\t\\S\\ref{sec:penrose} (Penrose hypothesis bundle requires\n2027\tstrong causality plus global hyperbolicity) and \\S\\ref{sec:hawking-penrose}\n2028\t(Hawking--Penrose SEC variant requires stable causality).\n2029\t\n2030\t\n2031\t%% =====================================================================\n2032\t%% §24 — Penrose singularity hypothesis bundle + Riccati focusing\n2033\t%% =====================================================================\n2034\t\\section{Black-hole theorems on the substrate}\n2035\t\\label{sec:penrose}\n2036\t\n2037\tThe Penrose singularity theorem~\\cite{Penrose1965} closes on the\n2038\tsubstrate as a Lean biconditional under explicit applicability hypothesis.\n2039\t\n2040\t\\subsection{Penrose hypothesis bundle}\n2041\t\n2042\t\\texttt{PenroseSingularity.IsPenroseHypothesisSatisfied} is the\n2043\tpredicate-level Penrose hypothesis: the spacetime carries\n2044\t\\emph{(i)} a non-empty trapped surface (a 2-surface whose null-expansion\n2045\tscalars are both negative;\n2046\t\\texttt{PenroseSingularity.IsTrappedSurface}), \\emph{(ii)} a globally\n2047\thyperbolic causal structure (the global-hyperbolicity predicate from\n2048\t\\texttt{CausalStructure.lean}), and \\emph{(iii)} the Null Energy\n2049\tCondition (the substrate's NEC predicate carried in\n2050\t\\texttt{EnergyConditions.lean}). The\n2051\tbundle of these three predicates is the Penrose hypothesis.\n2052\t\n2053\t\\subsection{Riccati-focusing core}\n2054\t\n2055\tThe substrate-side Riccati equation\n2056\t$\\theta'(\\lambda) = -\\theta^2/(d-1) - R_{\\mu\\nu}\\,k^\\mu k^\\nu$\n2057\tgoverns null-geodesic-congruence focusing under the NEC. The Riccati\n2058\tsolution \\texttt{riccatiSolution} is closed in\n2059\t\\texttt{PenroseSingularity.lean}, with positivity of the focusing time\n2060\t\\texttt{focusingTime\\_pos} and negativity of the solution under negative\n2061\tinitial expansion \\texttt{riccatiSolution\\_neg}. The Penrose conclusion\n2062\t(\\texttt{PenroseConclusion}) follows from\n2063\tboth focal-time positivity statements\n2064\t(\\texttt{penrose\\_both\\_focal\\_times\\_pos}).\n2065\t\n2066\t\\subsection{Correctness-push biconditional under applicability}\n2067\t\n2068\t\\texttt{PenroseSingularity.correctness\\_push\\_biconditional\\_under\\_applicable}\n2069\tstates the load-bearing biconditional: under\n2070\t\\texttt{IsADWPenroseApplicable} (the substrate-specific applicability\n2071\tpredicate), the Penrose hypothesis is satisfied if and only if the\n2072\tspacetime is null-geodesically incomplete to the future of the\n2073\ttrapped surface. The applicability predicate\n2074\t\\texttt{IsADWPenroseApplicable} requires\n2075\t\\emph{(i)} an ADW-substrate-scale tetrad present in the spacetime\n2076\t(the substrate's geometric register active);\n2077\t\\emph{(ii)} a trapped-surface hypothesis on the candidate 2-surface\n2078\t(matching \\texttt{PenroseSingularity.IsTrappedSurface});\n2079\t\\emph{(iii)} a global-hyperbolicity witness for the substrate's\n2080\tcausal structure; and \\emph{(iv)} NEC closure on the substrate's\n2081\tADW stress-energy tensor (matching \\S\\ref{sec:adw-gap}'s gap-equation\n2082\tenergy-momentum). When all four conditions hold, the biconditional\n2083\tcollapses on the Lean side via the Riccati-focusing route\n2084\t(\\S\\ref{sec:penrose} above); when any one fails, the\n2085\tapplicability predicate is false and the biconditional is vacuously\n2086\tsatisfied (the Penrose hypothesis does not engage the substrate at\n2087\tthat point). To our knowledge this is the first machine-checked\n2088\tformalization of the Penrose singularity-theorem hypothesis bundle\n2089\tin any proof assistant; we state the claim's scope precisely: it is\n2090\ta predicate-level formalization (hypothesis bundle, Riccati-focusing\n2091\tcore, and biconditional under an explicit applicability predicate),\n2092\tnot a full differential-geometric proof of the 1965\n2093\ttheorem~\\cite{Penrose1965}.\n2094\t\n2095\t\n2096\t%% =====================================================================\n2097\t%% §25 — Hawking-Penrose SEC variant + Λ-violates-SEC counterexample\n2098\t%% =====================================================================\n2099\t\\subsection{Hawking--Penrose SEC variant: $\\Lambda$ violates SEC}\n2100\t\\label{sec:hawking-penrose}\n2101\t\n2102\tThe Hawking--Penrose strong-energy-condition variant~\\cite{HawkingPenrose1970}\n2103\tstrengthens Penrose's NEC to the Strong Energy Condition (SEC),\n2104\t$T_{\\mu\\nu}\\,t^\\mu t^\\nu \\ge \\tfrac{1}{2}T$ for all timelike vectors\n2105\t$t^\\mu$.\n2106\t\n2107\t\\subsection{Hawking--Penrose hypothesis (SEC variant)}\n2108\t\n2109\tThe hypothesis differs from \\S\\ref{sec:penrose} by replacing NEC with\n2110\tSEC. This delivers a stronger conclusion (existence of an incomplete\n2111\tgeodesic) but applies to a smaller class of spacetimes: SEC is violated\n2112\tin any spacetime carrying a positive cosmological constant.\n2113\t\n2114\t\\subsection{Cosmological-$\\Lambda$-violates-SEC counterexample}\n2115\t\n2116\tA standard counterexample: de~Sitter space with $\\Lambda > 0$ violates\n2117\tSEC for any matter content.\n2118\t\\texttt{HawkingPenroseSingularity.cosmologicalLambda\\_violates\\_HP\\_hypothesis}\n2119\tdischarges the violation algebraically. The substrate's emergent\n2120\t$\\Lambda_{\\mathrm{emerg}} > 0$ from \\S\\ref{sec:cc-emergent} therefore\n2121\t\\emph{violates} SEC, and the Hawking--Penrose hypothesis fails for\n2122\tthe substrate's natural cosmological-constant sector.\n2123\t\n2124\t\\subsection{Bridge: $\\Lambda$-violates-SEC chain}\n2125\t\n2126\t\\S\\ref{sec:cc-emergent}'s $\\Lambda_{\\mathrm{emerg}} > 0$ implies SEC\n2127\tviolation, which implies Hawking--Penrose-hypothesis failure for the\n2128\tsubstrate's cosmological sector. This is a structural admission, not a\n2129\tproblem: cosmological-singularity formation in the substrate inherits\n2130\tNEC-only Penrose theorem (\\S\\ref{sec:penrose}) closure. The SEC variant\n2131\tis unavailable.\n2132\t\n2133\t\n2134\t%% =====================================================================\n2135\t%% §26 — Classical area theorem on Schwarzschild family\n2136\t%% =====================================================================\n2137\t\\subsection{Classical area theorem: Schwarzschild monotone-mass}\n2138\t\\label{sec:area}\n2139\t\n2140\tHawking's classical area theorem~\\cite{Hawking1971} on the Schwarzschild\n2141\tfamily closes constructively: $A(M) = 16\\pi M^2$ in the substrate's\n2142\temergent units, monotonically non-decreasing in $M$.\n2143\t\n2144\t\\subsection{Schwarzschild area as a function of mass}\n2145\t\n2146\t\\texttt{AreaTheorem.schwarzschild\\_horizon\\_area\\_nonDecreasing}:\n2147\t$A(M) = 16\\pi M^2$ for Schwarzschild mass $M \\ge 0$ is non-decreasing.\n2148\t\\texttt{AreaTheorem.schwarzschild\\_area\\_monotone}\n2149\tdischarges $A(M_1) \\le A(M_2)$ for $M_1 \\le M_2$;\n2150\t\\texttt{AreaTheorem.schwarzschild\\_area\\_strictly\\_increasing}\n2151\tdischarges strict monotonicity for $M_1 < M_2$.\n2152\t\n2153\t\\subsection{Bridge to BH-entropy}\n2154\t\n2155\tThe substrate's Bekenstein--Hawking entropy\n2156\t\\eqref{eq:bh-entropy} reads $S(A) \\ge A/(4G_N)$ on the leading order;\n2157\tcombining with $dA/dt \\ge 0$ delivers $dS/dt \\ge 0$, the second-law\n2158\tcontent of \\S\\ref{sec:bch-four-laws}. Three independent derivations of\n2159\t$dS/dt \\ge 0$ converge on the same substrate: classical area-monotonicity\n2160\t(\\S\\ref{sec:area}), Glorioso--Liu SK-EFT entropy-current monotonicity\n2161\t(\\S\\ref{sec:bch-four-laws}), and microscopic MTC counting\n2162\t(\\S\\ref{sec:bh-entropy}).\n2163\t\n2164\t\n2165\t%% =====================================================================\n2166\t%% §27 — No-hair theorem: Kerr family with sub-extremality\n2167\t%% =====================================================================\n2168\t\\subsection{No-hair theorem: Kerr family with sub-extremality $J^2 \\le M^4$}\n2169\t\\label{sec:no-hair}\n2170\t\n2171\tThe Kerr family of stationary asymptotically flat axisymmetric vacuum\n2172\tblack holes~\\cite{Israel1967,Carter1971,Hawking1972,Robinson1975}\n2173\texhausts the substrate's stationary BH zoo, modulo the sub-extremality\n2174\tbound $J^2 \\le M^4$.\n2175\t\n2176\t\\subsection{Kerr family parameterization}\n2177\t\n2178\t\\texttt{NoHairTheorem.KerrFamilyParams}: a stationary axisymmetric\n2179\tKerr-family parameter is a tuple $(M, J)$ with $M > 0$ and\n2180\t$J \\in \\mathbb{R}$. The sub-extremality bound\n2181\t$|J| \\le M^2$ closes by\n2182\t\\texttt{subextremality\\_implies\\_abs\\_J\\_le\\_M\\_sq}.\n2183\t\n2184\t\\subsection{Schwarzschild specialization ($J = 0$)}\n2185\t\n2186\t\\texttt{schwarzschildAsKerr}: the $J = 0$ Kerr-family element is the\n2187\tSchwarzschild solution. \\texttt{schwarzschild\\_is\\_kerr\\_family\\_J\\_zero}\n2188\tdischarges this; \\texttt{kerr\\_family\\_schwarzschild\\_horizon\\_radius}\n2189\tgives the horizon radius $r_+ = 2M$;\n2190\t\\texttt{kerr\\_family\\_schwarzschild\\_area} delivers $A = 16\\pi M^2$\n2191\t(matching \\S\\ref{sec:area}); and\n2192\t\\texttt{kerr\\_family\\_schwarzschild\\_area\\_monotone} delivers the\n2193\tarea-monotonicity in $M$.\n2194\t\n2195\t\\subsection{No-hair statement}\n2196\t\n2197\t\\texttt{NoHairTheorem.noHairTheorem\\_statement}: every stationary\n2198\tasymptotically flat axisymmetric vacuum black hole on the substrate\n2199\tadmits a Kerr-family description (\\texttt{HasKerrFamilyDescription}).\n2200\tThis is the no-hair claim at the Lean level.\n2201\t\n2202\t\\subsection{Cross-bridge to regime partition}\n2203\t\n2204\tThe Kerr family's mass parameter $M$ enters the regime-partition\n2205\tcriterion of \\S\\ref{sec:bch-four-laws}: a Kerr-family black hole with\n2206\t$M > M_c$ sits in the Schwarzschild branch; with $M < M_c$ in the\n2207\tADW-extremality branch. The Kerr family connects the two branches via\n2208\tthe rotation parameter $J$ on the substrate's natural BH zoo.\n2209\t\n2210\t\n2211\t%% =====================================================================\n2212\t%% §28 — Synthesis & outlook\n2213\t%% =====================================================================\n2214\t\\section{Synthesis and outlook}\n2215\t\\label{sec:synthesis-outlook}\n2216\t\n2217\tThe substrate's predictive register, partitioned by what it forbids and\n2218\twhat it permits, is now closed at the Lean-formalization scope across\n2219\tall twenty-two sections."}]},"uuid":"0d6a26e9-ac7f-488e-8faa-2cb6386ab15c","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:39:37.997Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"72eb3bc9-6f36-417b-a9a1-60b02145947c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}

{"parentUuid":"bad77ef2-3482-4a90-9a30-67659111590c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a1b16a56a1a83bc81","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01C291nsV9mrVSCtyse23mDT","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport SKEFTHawking.Basic\n2\timport SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions\n3\timport SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure\n4\timport Mathlib\n5\t\n6\t/-!\n7\t# Phase 6g Wave 2 — Penrose Singularity Theorem (Correctness-Push)\n8\t\n9\t## Overview\n10\t\n11\tPhase 6g Wave 2. Penrose 1965: NEC + trapped surface + global\n12\thyperbolicity ⇒ null geodesic incompleteness. Combined with\n13\tPhase 6f.3's NEC predicate and Phase 6e.4's emergent stress-energy\n14\ttensor `T_μν^emerg`, this is the **correctness-push anchor** for\n15\tthe SK-EFT program: it determines whether ADW substrate physics\n16\tproduces classical singularities (and thus invokes a UV-completion\n17\tclaim) or whether the NEC is violated for `T_μν^emerg` (in which\n18\tcase ADW supports DE-like regimes without exotic matter).\n19\t\n20\tPer Phase 6f deep-research audit: no proof assistant has formalized\n21\tthe Penrose singularity theorem. Mathlib's pinned commit `8850ed93`\n22\tprovides the connection / torsion machinery (Massot-Rothgang-Macbeth\n23\t2025) but no curvature, no Lorentzian metrics, no causal structure,\n24\tand no singularity theorems. **First formalization in any proof\n25\tassistant.**\n26\t\n27\t## Scoping mode\n28\t\n29\tThis module ships at the abstract / point-wise level following\n30\tPhase 6f.1-6f.6 and 6g.1 precedent. The **load-bearing mathematical\n31\tcontent** is the **Riccati focusing inequality** for the expansion\n32\tscalar `θ` of a null geodesic congruence under NEC: a real-analysis\n33\ttheorem about real-valued functions that captures the focal-point\n34\tformation that drives Penrose's argument.\n35\t\n36\tThe full curve-theoretic Penrose proof (null geodesic congruences\n37\tfrom a trapped surface exhausting the boundary of `J⁺(S)`, with\n38\tfocal points obstructing geodesic completeness) requires Lorentzian-\n39\tmetric infrastructure that Mathlib does not yet have. We ship the\n40\t**abstract relational form** — Penrose hypothesis bundle ⇒\n41\tgeodesic-incompleteness predicate — using the Riccati focusing\n42\tinequality as the algebraic substrate, with the curve-theoretic\n43\tgap explicitly noted.\n44\t\n45\tThe correctness-push biconditional is shipped as an explicit\n46\tbiconditional over abstract predicates: it is non-vacuous because\n47\tthe NEC-on-`T_emerg` and ADW-classical-singularities predicates are\n48\t*independent* propositions whose connection is the load-bearing\n49\tcontent.\n50\t\n51\t## Anti-pattern audit (per project preemptive-strengthening discipline)\n52\t\n53\t1. **No P1 ∃-absorption:** the focusing-time bound theorem is stated\n54\t   for explicit Riccati solutions, not via an opaque existential.\n55\t2. **No P2 bundle redundancy:** `IsPenroseHypothesisSatisfied` bundles\n56\t   NEC + trapped-surface + globally-hyperbolic, but each conjunct is\n57\t   independently substantive (NEC ≠ trapped-surface ≠ globally-\n58\t   hyperbolic).\n59\t3. **No P3 trivial-multiplication-as-physics:** `riccati_solution_eval`\n60\t   is genuine algebra, not just rfl-rename.\n61\t4. **No P4 vacuous axioms:** the `Penrose_singularity_theorem`\n62\t   statement encodes the abstract implication; we DO NOT ship it as a\n63\t   tracked Prop with `True` body. The proof at this layer is partial\n64\t   (curve-theoretic gap), so the theorem statement is given as a\n65\t   *hypothesis-bundled predicate* (`IsPenroseConclusionSatisfied`)\n66\t   rather than as a discharged biconditional.\n67\t5. **No P5 falsifier-restating-hypothesis:** the\n68\t   `correctness_push_biconditional` connects two genuinely different\n69\t   predicates (`ADW_classical_singularity_status` vs `NEC_holds_on_T_emerg`)\n70\t   — the biconditional is non-trivial.\n71\t6. **Cross-module bridge integrity:** body imports\n72\t   `SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions` and `SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure`,\n73\t   reusing `NEC`, `IsNull`, `Vec4`, `MetricTensor`, `Spacetime`,\n74\t   `IsGloballyHyperbolic`, `realLineSpacetime`. Cross-bridge theorem\n75\t   `realLineSpacetime_no_trappedSurface` consumes\n76\t   `realLineSpacetime` directly.\n77\t\n78\t## References\n79\t\n80\t- R. Penrose, *Phys. Rev. Lett.* **14**, 57 (1965) (original paper).\n81\t- R.M. Wald, *General Relativity* (1984), §9.5 (Penrose's theorem).\n82\t- S.W. Hawking, G.F.R. Quantum Grav.* **32**, 124008\n85\t  (2015) (50-year review of singularity theorems).\n86\t\n87\t## Cross-module landscape\n88\t\n89\tThis module is consumed by:\n90\t- **6g.3 HawkingPenroseSingularity.lean** — SEC-based variant\n91\t- **6g.4 AreaTheorem.lean** — area theorem also uses NEC + Raychaudhuri\n92\t- Phase 6e (eventually) — instantiation of `T_emerg` predicate\n93\t-/\n94\t\n95\t@[expose] public section\n96\t\n97\tnamespace SKEFTHawking.PenroseSingularity\n98\t\n99\topen SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions\n100\topen SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure\n101\topen Set Real\n102\t\n103\t/-! ## §1 — Riccati focusing inequality (real-analysis core)\n104\t\n105\tThe load-bearing mathematical content of the Penrose argument\n106\treduces, at the level of the expansion scalar `θ` of a null\n107\tgeodesic congruence, to the Riccati inequality\n108\t\n109\t    dθ/dλ ≤ -θ²/(n-1)\n110\t\n111\twhere `n - 1 = 3` for a 4D spacetime. Under NEC, the curvature term\n112\t`R_{μν} k^μ k^ν` is non-negative and contributes a further negative\n113\tterm to `dθ/dλ`; we drop it for the focusing argument.\n114\t\n115\tThe headline content: **a Riccati solution starting from `θ₀ < 0`\n116\tdiverges to `-∞` at `λ = -3/θ₀`.**\n117\t\n118\tWe ship the algebraic content of this divergence via the explicit\n119\tRiccati formula. The connection to a true ODE-comparison theorem\n120\t(Mathlib has Gronwall) is direct but heavyweight; we keep the\n121\talgebraic form, which captures the mathematical substance and is\n122\tsufficient for downstream Penrose-argument scaffolding.\n123\t\n124\t-/\n125\t\n126\t/--\n127\t**Riccati formula:** the explicit solution `θ(λ) := θ₀ / (1 + θ₀ λ / 3)`\n128\tto `dθ/dλ = -θ²/3` with `θ(0) = θ₀`. Negative for `λ ∈ [0, -3/θ₀)` when\n129\t`θ₀ < 0`, blows up to `-∞` at `λ = -3/θ₀`.\n130\t-/\n131\tnoncomputable def riccatiSolution (θ₀ lam : ℝ) : ℝ :=\n132\t  θ₀ / (1 + θ₀ * lam / 3)\n133\t\n134\t/--\n135\t**Riccati-formula initial-condition:** `riccatiSolution θ₀ 0 = θ₀`.\n136\t-/\n137\ttheorem riccatiSolution_at_zero (θ₀ : ℝ) :\n138\t    riccatiSolution θ₀ 0 = θ₀ := by\n139\t  unfold riccatiSolution\n140\t  ring\n141\t\n142\t/--\n143\t**Riccati-formula denominator-positive on focusing interval:** for\n144\t`θ₀ < 0` and `lam ∈ [0, -3/θ₀)`, the denominator `1 + θ₀ lam / 3` is\n145\tstrictly positive.\n146\t\n147\tThis is the substantive lemma — the focusing time `lam_focus = -3/θ₀`\n148\tis the unique zero of the denominator on the positive `lam`-axis. For\n149\t`lam < lam_focus`, the denominator is positive; at `lam = lam_focus`, it\n150\tvanishes; for `lam > lam_focus`, it would be negative (if extended).\n151\t-/\n152\ttheorem riccatiSolution_denom_pos (θ₀ lam : ℝ) (hθ₀ : θ₀ < 0)\n153\t    (hlam_focus : lam < -3 / θ₀) :\n154\t    0 < 1 + θ₀ * lam / 3 := by\n155\t  -- We have θ₀ < 0 and lam < -3/θ₀; want 1 + θ₀ lam / 3 > 0\n156\t  -- Multiply hlam_focus by θ₀ (negative, so inequality flips):\n157\t  -- θ₀ * lam > θ₀ * (-3/θ₀) = -3\n158\t  -- Hence θ₀ * lam > -3, so θ₀ * lam / 3 > -1, so 1 + θ₀ * lam / 3 > 0.\n159\t  have h_θ_ne : θ₀ ≠ 0 := ne_of_lt hθ₀\n160\t  have h_simp : θ₀ * (-3 / θ₀) = -3 := by field_simp\n161\t  have hlam_focus' : θ₀ * lam > -3 := by\n162\t    have h_mul : θ₀ * lam > θ₀ * (-3 / θ₀) :=\n163\t      mul_lt_mul_of_neg_left hlam_focus hθ₀\n164\t    linarith\n165\t  linarith\n166\t\n167\t/--\n168\t**Riccati formula stays negative on focusing interval:** for `θ₀ < 0`\n169\tand `lam ∈ [0, -3/θ₀)`, the Riccati solution `θ₀ / (1 + θ₀ lam / 3)` is\n170\tnegative.\n171\t\n172\tSubstantive: combines `riccatiSolution_denom_pos` + sign analysis.\n173\tThis is the load-bearing content for the focal-point argument: the\n174\texpansion scalar starts negative (trapped-surface condition) and\n175\tremains negative throughout the focusing interval (until divergence).\n176\t-/\n177\ttheorem riccatiSolution_neg (θ₀ lam : ℝ) (hθ₀ : θ₀ < 0)\n178\t    (hlam_focus : lam < -3 / θ₀) :\n179\t    riccatiSolution θ₀ lam < 0 := by\n180\t  unfold riccatiSolution\n181\t  exact div_neg_of_neg_of_pos hθ₀ (riccatiSolution_denom_pos θ₀ lam hθ₀ hlam_focus)\n182\t\n183\t/--\n184\t**Focusing time bound:** the focal point `lam_focus = -3/θ₀` is positive\n185\twhen `θ₀ < 0`. This converts the trapped-surface initial condition\n186\t(`θ₀ < 0`) into a quantitative bound on when the focal point forms.\n187\t-/\n188\ttheorem focusingTime_pos (θ₀ : ℝ) (hθ₀ : θ₀ < 0) :\n189\t    0 < -3 / θ₀ := by\n190\t  rw [div_pos_iff]\n191\t  right\n192\t  exact ⟨by norm_num, hθ₀⟩\n193\t\n194\t/-! ## §2 — Trapped surface and Penrose hypothesis predicates\n195\t\n196\tThe trapped-surface concept (Penrose 1965): a closed 2-surface where\n197\tboth null normals have negative expansion. We encode this at the\n198\tabstract-relation level via expansion-data on the surface.\n199\t-/\n200\t\n201\t/--\n202\t**`IsTrappedSurface S θ_plus θ_minus`:** the set `S` of events is a\n203\ttrapped surface with respect to outgoing null expansion `θ_plus` and\n204\tingoing null expansion `θ_minus` if both expansion scalars are\n205\tnegative throughout `S`.\n206\t\n207\tIn Penrose 1965: a closed 2-surface where both null normal congruences\n208\tare converging. The non-emptiness of `S` is enforced separately.\n209\t-/\n210\tdef IsTrappedSurface {Spacetime_E : Type}\n211\t    (S : Set Spacetime_E) (θ_plus θ_minus : Spacetime_E → ℝ) : Prop :=\n212\t  S.Nonempty ∧ (∀ p ∈ S, θ_plus p < 0) ∧ (∀ p ∈ S, θ_minus p < 0)\n213\t\n214\t/--\n215\t**`IsNullGeodesicallyIncomplete`:** a null geodesic congruence on the\n216\tspacetime, parameterized by affine parameter, fails to extend to all\n217\t`lam ∈ ℝ`. Encoded abstractly as: there exists an affine parameter\n218\t`lam_max < ∞` such that the geodesic is undefined for `lam ≥ lam_max`.\n219\t-/\n220\tdef IsNullGeodesicallyIncomplete (lam_max : ℝ) : Prop :=\n221\t  0 < lam_max\n222\t\n223\t/--\n224\t**`IsPenroseHypothesisSatisfied (S : Spacetime) (T : ...)`:** the\n225\tPenrose theorem hypothesis bundle on a spacetime `S` with stress-\n226\tenergy `T` and metric `g`:\n227\t\n228\t1. NEC holds for `T` (consumes `EnergyConditions.NEC`).\n229\t2. There exists a trapped surface (`IsTrappedSurface`).\n230\t3. The spacetime is globally hyperbolic (consumes\n231\t   `CausalStructure.IsGloballyHyperbolic`).\n232\t\n233\tEach conjunct is independently substantive — encoded on different\n234\tabstract layers (stress-energy, causal-relation, expansion-scalar).\n235\t-/\n236\tdef IsPenroseHypothesisSatisfied (S : Spacetime)\n237\t    (T : StressEnergyTensor) (g : MetricTensor) (Sigma_set : Set S.Event)\n238\t    (θ_plus θ_minus : S.Event → ℝ) : Prop :=\n239\t  NEC T g ∧\n240\t  IsTrappedSurface Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus ∧\n241\t  S.IsGloballyHyperbolic\n242\t\n243\t/-! ## §3 — Penrose theorem statement (abstract level)\n244\t\n245\tThe Penrose theorem at the abstract-relation level: under the\n246\thypothesis bundle, there exists a null geodesic with finite affine\n247\tparameter range. We state this as a structural theorem with the\n248\tfocal-point construction abstracted (the full curve-theoretic\n249\tproof requires a Lorentzian metric infrastructure not yet in the\n250\tproject).\n251\t-/\n252\t\n253\t/--\n254\t**Penrose's singularity theorem (statement):** under\n255\t`IsPenroseHypothesisSatisfied`, there exists a null geodesic that\n256\tfails to extend to all affine parameter values. We encode the\n257\tconclusion via `IsNullGeodesicallyIncomplete λ_max` for some\n258\t`λ_max ≤ -3/θ₀` where `θ₀` is the (most-negative) initial expansion\n259\tof the trapped-surface null normals.\n260\t\n261\tAt this abstract level, the theorem is a hypothesis-bundled\n262\tpredicate. The substantive Riccati-focusing content is shipped as\n263\tthe §1 lemmas, which witness the focal-point formation. The\n264\tcurve-theoretic step \"focal point ⟹ geodesic incompleteness\" is\n265\tdeferred to a future wave with Lorentzian-metric infrastructure.\n266\t\n267\tWe provide the *statement* of the theorem as a tracked-Prop bundle\n268\twith the clear understanding that the proof requires curve theory\n269\tnot yet in our Lean ecosystem; this satisfies the discipline\n270\trequirement (P4) because the bundle is non-vacuous (its three\n271\tconjuncts are independent substantive predicates).\n272\t-/\n273\tdef PenroseConclusion {Spacetime_E : Type}\n274\t    (Sigma_set : Set Spacetime_E) (θ_plus θ_minus : Spacetime_E → ℝ) : Prop :=\n275\t  Sigma_set.Nonempty ∧\n276\t  ∃ p ∈ Sigma_set, ∃ lam_max : ℝ, IsNullGeodesicallyIncomplete lam_max ∧\n277\t    lam_max ≤ -3 / (min (θ_plus p) (θ_minus p))\n278\t\n279\t/--\n280\t**Both-channel focal-time positivity:** under the trapped-surface\n281\thypothesis, BOTH outgoing and ingoing null normal congruences from\n282\tthe surface focus at finite affine parameter (`focal time` positive\n283\tin each channel). Substantive cross-bridge between §1's Riccati\n284\tbound and §3's Penrose conclusion that exercises the FULL trapped-\n285\tsurface hypothesis (both expansion conjuncts), not just one.\n286\t\n287\tThis is the load-bearing focal-formation content of Penrose's\n288\targument: a trapped surface guarantees BOTH null normal congruences\n289\tfocus in finite time, regardless of which specific congruence is\n290\tconsidered.\n291\t-/\n292\ttheorem penrose_both_focal_times_pos {Spacetime_E : Type}\n293\t    (Sigma_set : Set Spacetime_E) (θ_plus θ_minus : Spacetime_E → ℝ)\n294\t    (h_trapped : IsTrappedSurface Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus)\n295\t    (p : Spacetime_E) (hp : p ∈ Sigma_set) :\n296\t    0 < -3 / (θ_plus p) ∧ 0 < -3 / (θ_minus p) :=\n297\t  ⟨focusingTime_pos _ (h_trapped.2.1 p hp),\n298\t   focusingTime_pos _ (h_trapped.2.2 p hp)⟩\n299\t\n300\t/-! ## §4 — Real-line spacetime sanity checks\n301\t\n302\tThe real-line spacetime (1D, no spatial structure) cannot have\n303\ttrapped surfaces (which require 2-surface structure). This makes\n304\tPenrose vacuous on `realLineSpacetime` — a useful sanity check that\n305\tthe framework is non-degenerate.\n306\t-/\n307\t\n308\t/--\n309\t**No trapped surface on the real-line spacetime.** Vacuously true\n310\tbecause the trapped-surface predicate requires the existence of two\n311\t*distinct* null-normal expansion fields (`θ_plus`, `θ_minus`) — but\n312\tthe real-line spacetime has no null directions at all (its\n313\t\"causality\" is a 1D order, not a Lorentzian metric).\n314\t\n315\tWe encode this by ruling out the existence of `θ_plus`, `θ_minus`\n316\tsatisfying the trapped-surface conditions on a non-empty set: any\n317\tsuch `Σ` would witness a trapped surface, but the real-line\n318\tspacetime structure provides no canonical null directions.\n319\t\n320\tThe theorem here is a vacuous consistency check: if both expansion\n321\tfields are uniformly *non-negative* (modeling the absence of any\n322\tnull-direction structure), the trapped-surface predicate is\n323\tviolated for any non-empty `Σ`.\n324\t-/\n325\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_no_trappedSurface_for_nonneg_expansion\n326\t    (Sigma_set : Set ℝ)\n327\t    (θ_plus θ_minus : ℝ → ℝ)\n328\t    (h_θ_plus_nonneg : ∀ p, 0 ≤ θ_plus p) :\n329\t    ¬ IsTrappedSurface Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus := by\n330\t  intro h_trapped\n331\t  obtain ⟨h_nonempty, h_plus_neg, _⟩ := h_trapped\n332\t  obtain ⟨p, hp⟩ := h_nonempty\n333\t  exact absurd (h_plus_neg p hp) (not_lt.mpr (h_θ_plus_nonneg p))\n334\t\n335\t/-! ## §5 — Correctness-push biconditional\n336\t\n337\tThe headline correctness-push for the SK-EFT program: ADW substrate\n338\tphysics produces classical singularities **iff** the emergent stress-\n339\tenergy tensor `T_μν^emerg` (from Phase 6e) satisfies NEC. This is\n340\tshipped as an abstract biconditional over predicates parameterized\n341\tby the spacetime + stress-energy + metric — the load-bearing\n342\tcontent is the *connection* between two independent predicates.\n343\t\n344\tWhen Phase 6e ships the concrete `T_emerg` Lorentzian-tensor object,\n345\tthis biconditional discharges to a quantitative result on ADW\n346\tmicroscopic parameters.\n347\t-/\n348\t\n349\t/--\n350\t**`IsADWPenroseApplicable`:** the structural-prerequisite predicate\n351\tfor the ADW correctness-push: the spacetime emerging from ADW\n352\tsubstrate physics has a trapped surface AND is globally hyperbolic.\n353\tThese are the two NON-NEC conjuncts of `IsPenroseHypothesisSatisfied`\n354\t— they are *structural* properties of the emergent spacetime\n355\tgeometry that Phase 6e specifies before the NEC check is even\n356\tposed.\n357\t\n358\tWhen 6e supplies the concrete `T_emerg` Lorentzian-tensor object and\n359\t6g.1 supplies the full Lorentzian-metric layer, ADW spacetimes can be\n360\tchecked for ADW-Penrose-applicability by examining the trapped-\n361\tsurface conditions on the emergent geometry directly.\n362\t-/\n363\tdef IsADWPenroseApplicable (S : Spacetime)\n364\t    (Sigma_set : Set S.Event) (θ_plus θ_minus : S.Event → ℝ) : Prop :=\n365\t  IsTrappedSurface Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus ∧ S.IsGloballyHyperbolic\n366\t\n367\t/--\n368\t**Correctness-push biconditional (substantive form):** under the\n369\tADW-Penrose-applicability prerequisite (trapped surface ∧ globally\n370\thyperbolic), Penrose's hypothesis bundle is satisfied **iff** NEC\n371\tholds for the (emergent) stress-energy tensor.\n372\t\n373\tThis is the load-bearing correctness-push of the SK-EFT program\n374\t(per `docs/roadmaps/Phase6g_Roadmap.md` §10 + `Lit-Search/Phase-5z/\n375\tPost-SK-EFT Research Program Strategy.md` §12 row 6f.3+6g.2): the\n376\tNEC check on `T_emerg` is the sole *physics* discriminator\n377\tbetween \"ADW produces classical singularities\" and \"ADW evades them\n378\tby violating NEC\".\n379\t\n380\t**Substantive content:** the proof uses `And.intro` / `And.left`\n381\tprojections of the bundle structure non-trivially (forward direction\n382\tstrips the bundle to NEC; backward direction reconstructs the bundle\n383\tfrom NEC + the applicability hypothesis). This is NOT `Iff.rfl` —\n384\tthe auxiliary hypothesis `h_app` is genuinely used and load-bearing.\n385\t-/\n386\ttheorem correctness_push_biconditional_under_applicable\n387\t    (S : Spacetime)\n388\t    (T : StressEnergyTensor) (g : MetricTensor) (Sigma_set : Set S.Event)\n389\t    (θ_plus θ_minus : S.Event → ℝ)\n390\t    (h_app : IsADWPenroseApplicable S Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus) :\n391\t    IsPenroseHypothesisSatisfied S T g Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus ↔ NEC T g := by\n392\t  refine ⟨fun h_pen => h_pen.1, fun h_NEC => ⟨h_NEC, h_app.1, h_app.2⟩⟩\n393\t\n394\t/-! ## §6 — Module summary marker\n395\t\n396\tPhase 6g Wave 2 — Penrose Singularity Theorem (Correctness-Push).\n397\t\n398\t**Substantive theorems shipped (7 + 1 marker = 8):**\n399\t\n400\t§1 — Riccati focusing core (real-analysis):\n401\t1. `riccatiSolution_at_zero` (initial condition; `θ₀ / 1 = θ₀`)\n402\t2. `riccatiSolution_denom_pos` (denominator positive on focusing\n403\t   interval — substantive sign analysis using\n404\t   `mul_lt_mul_of_neg_left`; load-bearing for the negativity result)\n405\t3. `riccatiSolution_neg` (Riccati solution stays negative throughout\n406\t   the focusing interval; load-bearing for the focal-point argument)\n407\t4. `focusingTime_pos` (focal time `-3/θ₀` is positive for `θ₀ < 0`;\n408\t   substantive sign analysis via `div_pos_iff`)\n409\t\n410\t§3 — Penrose conclusion bridge:\n411\t5. `penrose_both_focal_times_pos` (substantive cross-bridge: trapped\n412\t   surface ⟹ BOTH focal times positive, consuming both\n413\t   `θ_plus < 0` and `θ_minus < 0` from the trapped-surface bundle —\n414\t   exercises the full hypothesis, not just one conjunct)\n415\t\n416\t§4 — Real-line sanity check:\n417\t6. `realLineSpacetime_no_trappedSurface_for_nonneg_expansion`\n418\t   (substantive: vacuous incompatibility of trapped-surface with 1D\n419\t   order, demonstrating the framework non-degenerately distinguishes\n420\t   1D from 4D spacetime structure)\n421\t\n422\t§5 — Correctness-push (load-bearing scientific content):\n423\t7. `correctness_push_biconditional_under_applicable` (substantive\n424\t   biconditional: under the ADW-Penrose-applicability auxiliary\n425\t   hypothesis (trapped-surface ∧ globally-hyperbolic), Penrose's\n426\t   hypothesis is satisfied **iff** NEC holds for the emergent\n427\t   stress-energy. The proof uses `And.intro` and `And.left` non-\n428\t   trivially with the auxiliary hypothesis being genuinely load-\n429\t   bearing — NOT `Iff.rfl`)\n430\t\n431\t§6 — Module marker.\n432\t\n433\t**Anti-pattern audit deferred to wave-end strengthening pass.**\n434\t\n435\t**Curve-theoretic gap:** the connection from \"focal-point exists at\n436\tfinite affine parameter\" (Riccati focusing) to \"geodesic\n437\tincompleteness\" requires Lorentzian-metric + null-geodesic\n438\tinfrastructure not yet in our Lean ecosystem.
