Hawking-Penrose 1970: SEC + generic condition +\n13\ttrapped surface (timelike or null) + globally hyperbolic ⇒ geodesic\n14\tincompleteness. The SEC-based variant of Penrose's theorem; broader\n15\tscope (covers both cosmological singularities — big bang in FLRW —\n16\tand gravitational-collapse singularities — BH interiors).\n17\t\n18\tPer Phase 6f deep-research audit: no proof assistant has formalized\n19\tthe Hawking-Penrose theorem. **First formalization in any proof\n20\tassistant.**\n21\t\n22\t## Scoping mode\n23\t\n24\tSame algebraic / abstract-relation level as 6g.2. The load-bearing\n25\tmathematical content is the SEC-variant Riccati focusing inequality\n26\t(timelike geodesic congruences):\n27\t\n28\t    dθ/dλ ≤ -θ²/3 - σ² + ω² - R_{μν} t^μ t^ν\n29\t\n30\tFor an irrotational (ω = 0) timelike congruence under SEC\n31\t(`R_{μν} t^μ t^ν ≥ 0` for timelike `t`), this reduces to the same\n32\tRiccati form as 6g.2. We re-export the §1 focusing core from 6g.2\n33\t(`riccatiSolution_neg`, `focusingTime_pos`) directly.\n34\t\n35\tThe Hawking-Penrose theorem differs from Penrose in:\n36\t- **Energy condition:** SEC instead of NEC.\n37\t- **Trapped surface:** can be either null (Penrose) or timelike\n38\t  (Hawking-Penrose adds the timelike case for cosmological + BH\n39\t  applications).\n40\t- **Generic condition:** at least one point along every timelike /\n41\t  null geodesic where `R_{μν} v^μ v^ν > 0` (strict, not just ≥ 0).\n42\t  Avoids the degenerate flat-spacetime case.\n43\t\n44\t## Anti-pattern audit (per project preemptive-strengthening discipline)\n45\t\n46\t1. **No P1 ∃-absorption:** counterexample witnesses use explicit\n47\t   tensors (cosmological-Λ violates SEC, consumed via 6f.3\n48\t   `cosmologicalLambda_violates_SEC`).\n49\t2. **No P2 bundle redundancy:** `IsHawkingPenroseHypothesisSatisfied`\n50\t   bundles SEC + trapped + globally-hyperbolic, but each conjunct is\n51\t   independently substantive.\n52\t3. **No P3 trivial-multiplication-as-physics:** the substantive\n53\t   theorems exercise either Riccati sign analysis (§1 re-exports) or\n54\t   counterexample witnesses (cosmological-Λ).\n55\t4. **No P4 vacuous axioms:** the Hawking-Penrose statement bundle is\n56\t   non-vacuous (its predicates are independent — SEC and trapped-\n57\t   surface are both genuine conditions).\n58\t5. **No P5 falsifier-restating-hypothesis:** the\n59\t   `cosmologicalLambda_violates_HP_hypothesis` theorem witnesses a\n60\t   genuine non-implication (cosmological Λ-fluid evades HP because it\n61\t   violates SEC at every Λ > 0). The substantive content is the\n62\t   call to 6f.3's `cosmologicalLambda_violates_SEC`.\n63\t6. **Cross-module bridge integrity:** body imports\n64\t   `SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions`, `SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure`,\n65\t   `SKEFTHawking.PenroseSingularity` and *calls* `SEC`,\n66\t   `cosmologicalLambdaTensor`, `cosmologicalLambda_violates_SEC`,\n67\t   `IsTrappedSurface`, `realLineSpacetime`, `focusingTime_pos`.\n68\t\n69\t## References\n70\t\n71\t- S.W. Hawking, R. Penrose, *Proc. R. Soc. London A* **314**, 529\n72\t  (1970) (original paper).\n73\t- R.M. Wald, *General Relativity* (1984), §9.5 (singularity theorems).\n74\t- S.W. Hawking, G.F.R. Ellis, *The Large Scale Structure of Space-\n75\t  Time* (1973), §8.2.\n76\t- J.M.M. Senovilla, D. Garfinkle, *Class. Quantum Grav.* **32**, 124008\n77\t  (2015) (50-year review).\n78\t\n79\t## Cross-module landscape\n80\t\n81\tThis module is consumed by:\n82\t- **6g.4 AreaTheorem.lean** — area theorem uses overlapping focusing\n83\t  inequalities under NEC (no direct dependency, but conceptual\n84\t  parallel)\n85\t- Phase 6e (eventually) — instantiation of `T_emerg` SEC check\n86\t- Cosmological-singularity discussions throughout the program\n87\t-/\n88\t\n89\t@[expose] public section\n90\t\n91\tnamespace SKEFTHawking.HawkingPenroseSingularity\n92\t\n93\topen SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions\n94\topen SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure\n95\topen SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure.Spacetime\n96\topen SKEFTHawking.PenroseSingularity\n97\topen Set Real\n98\t\n99\t/-! ## §1 — Hawking-Penrose hypothesis predicates\n100\t\n101\tThe Hawking-Penrose hypothesis bundle: SEC + (timelike OR null)\n102\ttrapped surface + globally hyperbolic.\n103\t\n104\tWe REUSE `IsTrappedSurface` from 6g.2 because the trapped-surface\n105\tpredicate is signature-agnostic at our abstract-relation level\n106\t(both null and timelike trapped surfaces have negative expansions\n107\talong their respective normal congruences).\n108\t-/\n109\t\n110\t/--\n111\t**`IsHawkingPenroseHypothesisSatisfied`:** SEC bundle for the\n112\tHawking-Penrose theorem. Components:\n113\t1. SEC holds for `T` (consumes `EnergyConditions.SEC` with trace\n114\t   parameter `trT : ℝ` and timelike direction `t : Vec4`).\n115\t2. There exists a trapped surface (timelike or null variant; we use\n116\t   the null-variant `IsTrappedSurface` from 6g.2 with the generic-\n117\t   condition role absorbed into the trapped-surface conjunct).\n118\t3. The spacetime is globally hyperbolic.\n119\t-/\n120\tdef IsHawkingPenroseHypothesisSatisfied (S : Spacetime)\n121\t    (T : StressEnergyTensor) (g : MetricTensor)\n122\t    (t_dir : Vec4) (trT : ℝ)\n123\t    (Sigma_set : Set S.Event)\n124\t    (θ_plus θ_minus : S.Event → ℝ) : Prop :=\n125\t  SEC T g t_dir trT ∧\n126\t  IsTrappedSurface Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus ∧\n127\t  S.IsGloballyHyperbolic\n128\t\n129\t/-! ## §2 — Substantive correctness-push under applicability\n130\t\n131\tMirrors 6g.2's `correctness_push_biconditional_under_applicable`:\n132\tunder the structural-prerequisite hypothesis (trapped surface ∧\n133\tglobally hyperbolic), the Hawking-Penrose hypothesis bundle is\n134\tsatisfied **iff** SEC holds.\n135\t\n136\tThis is the load-bearing logical bridge for SEC-driven correctness\n137\tpush (e.g., for cosmological-singularity questions in the SK-EFT\n138\tprogram: does ADW-emergent gravity satisfy SEC at high curvature?).\n139\t-/\n140\t\n141\t/--\n142\t**Hawking-Penrose biconditional under applicability:** under the\n143\tADW-Penrose-applicability auxiliary hypothesis (which is *the same*\n144\tstructural prerequisite as for Penrose), the Hawking-Penrose\n145\thypothesis is satisfied **iff** SEC holds.\n146\t\n147\tThe substantive content is the bundle structure: forward extracts\n148\tSEC; backward reconstructs the bundle from SEC + the auxiliary\n149\thypothesis. NOT `Iff.rfl` — `h_app` is genuinely load-bearing.\n150\t-/\n151\ttheorem hawkingPenrose_biconditional_under_applicable\n152\t    (S : Spacetime)\n153\t    (T : StressEnergyTensor) (g : MetricTensor)\n154\t    (t_dir : Vec4) (trT : ℝ)\n155\t    (Sigma_set : Set S.Event)\n156\t    (θ_plus θ_minus : S.Event → ℝ)\n157\t    (h_app : IsADWPenroseApplicable S Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus) :\n158\t    IsHawkingPenroseHypothesisSatisfied S T g t_dir trT Sigma_set\n159\t        θ_plus θ_minus ↔ SEC T g t_dir trT := by\n160\t  refine ⟨fun h_hp => h_hp.1, fun h_SEC => ⟨h_SEC, h_app.1, h_app.2⟩⟩\n161\t\n162\t/-! ## §3 — Cosmological-Λ counterexample\n163\t\n164\tThe cosmological-Λ stress-energy tensor `T_μν = -Λ g_μν` (Λ > 0)\n165\tviolates SEC and therefore EVADES the Hawking-Penrose hypothesis:\n166\tde Sitter spacetime has no cosmological singularity, consistent\n167\twith eternal expansion.\n168\t\n169\tThis is the load-bearing physical content connecting our abstract\n170\tHP hypothesis to a concrete spacetime: de Sitter (Λ > 0) is\n171\tnon-singular precisely because it violates SEC, and HP requires SEC.\n172\t-/\n173\t\n174\t/--\n175\t**Cosmological-Λ violates the Hawking-Penrose hypothesis (Λ > 0):**\n176\tthe cosmological-Λ stress-energy tensor `-Λ g_μν` for `Λ > 0`\n177\tviolates SEC (consumes 6f.3 `cosmologicalLambda_violates_SEC`),\n178\thence it cannot satisfy the SEC conjunct of the HP hypothesis\n179\tbundle.\n180\t\n181\tSubstantive cross-bridge: this is the SEC half of the de Sitter\n182\tnon-singularity argument. The 6f.3 counterexample directly\n183\tdischarges this without re-deriving the Λ-violates-SEC algebra.\n184\t-/\n185\ttheorem cosmologicalLambda_violates_HP_hypothesis\n186\t    (S : Spacetime) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ : 0 < Λ)\n187\t    (Sigma_set : Set S.Event)\n188\t    (θ_plus θ_minus : S.Event → ℝ) :\n189\t    ¬ IsHawkingPenroseHypothesisSatisfied\n190\t        S\n191\t        (cosmologicalLambdaTensor Λ)\n192\t        minkowskiMetric\n193\t        ![1, 0, 0, 0]\n194\t        (-4 * Λ)\n195\t        Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus := by\n196\t  intro h_hp\n197\t  -- Extract SEC from the HP-hypothesis bundle\n198\t  have h_SEC : SEC (cosmologicalLambdaTensor Λ) minkowskiMetric\n199\t      ![1, 0, 0, 0] (-4 * Λ) := h_hp.1\n200\t  -- 6f.3's `cosmologicalLambda_violates_SEC` provides a witness vector\n201\t  -- v_witness := ![1,0,0,0] that is future-directed timelike but for\n202\t  -- which the SEC inequality fails. We apply h_SEC to the witness and\n203\t  -- contradict the negative-inequality fact from 6f.3.\n204\t  obtain ⟨h_fdtl, h_neg⟩ := cosmologicalLambda_violates_SEC hΛ\n205\t  exact h_neg (h_SEC ![1, 0, 0, 0] h_fdtl)\n206\t\n207\t/-! ## §4 — Real-line spacetime sanity check\n208\t\n209\tSame as 6g.2: the 1D real-line spacetime cannot host trapped surfaces\n210\tbecause there are no 2-surfaces. The HP hypothesis is therefore\n211\tvacuously refuted on `realLineSpacetime` by the same mechanism as\n212\tthe Penrose hypothesis.\n213\t-/\n214\t\n215\t/--\n216\t**Real-line spacetime cannot satisfy the HP hypothesis** (under the\n217\tnon-negative-expansion assumption ruling out non-existent null\n218\tdirections). Consumes\n219\t`realLineSpacetime_no_trappedSurface_for_nonneg_expansion` from 6g.2.\n220\t-/\n221\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_no_HP_hypothesis_for_nonneg_expansion\n222\t    (T : StressEnergyTensor) (g : MetricTensor)\n223\t    (t_dir : Vec4) (trT : ℝ)\n224\t    (Sigma_set : Set ℝ)\n225\t    (θ_plus θ_minus : ℝ → ℝ)\n226\t    (h_θ_plus_nonneg : ∀ p, 0 ≤ θ_plus p) :\n227\t    ¬ IsHawkingPenroseHypothesisSatisfied\n228\t        realLineSpacetime T g t_dir trT Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus := by\n229\t  intro h_hp\n230\t  exact realLineSpacetime_no_trappedSurface_for_nonneg_expansion\n231\t    Sigma_set θ_plus θ_minus h_θ_plus_nonneg h_hp.2.1\n232\t\n233\t/-! ## §5 — Re-export of 6g.2 focusing core\n234\t\n235\tThe Riccati focusing argument is identical for SEC-driven (timelike)\n236\tfocusing and NEC-driven (null) focusing — the divisor `n - 1 = 3`\n237\tin 4D is the same. We re-export the §1 focusing-time bound as a\n238\tnamed alias to confirm cross-module continuity.\n239\t-/\n240\t\n241\t/--\n242\t**Hawking-Penrose focusing-time bound** is identical to Penrose's:\n243\tthe focal time `λ_focus = -3/θ₀` is positive whenever the initial\n244\texpansion `θ₀ < 0`. Re-export of 6g.2 `focusingTime_pos` as a\n245\tsubstantive named consumer (audit P6 cross-module bridge).\n246\t-/\n247\ttheorem hawkingPenrose_focusingTime_pos (θ₀ : ℝ) (hθ₀ : θ₀ < 0) :\n248\t    0 < -3 / θ₀ :=\n249\t  focusingTime_pos θ₀ hθ₀\n250\t\n251\t/-! ## §6 — Module summary marker\n252\t\n253\tPhase 6g Wave 3 — Hawking-Penrose Singularity Theorem.\n254\t\n255\t**Substantive theorems shipped (4 + 1 marker = 5):**\n256\t\n257\t§2 — Correctness-push under applicability:\n258\t1. `hawkingPenrose_biconditional_under_applicable` (substantive\n259\t   biconditional under auxiliary hypothesis; `And.intro` /\n260\t   `And.left` non-trivially with `h_app` load-bearing — NOT\n261\t   `Iff.rfl`)\n262\t\n263\t§3 — Cosmological-Λ counterexample:\n264\t2. `cosmologicalLambda_violates_HP_hypothesis` (substantive\n265\t   cross-bridge to 6f.3's `cosmologicalLambda_violates_SEC`;\n266\t   load-bearing physical content for the de Sitter\n267\t   non-singularity argument)\n268\t\n269\t§4 — Real-line sanity check:\n270\t3. `realLineSpacetime_no_HP_hypothesis_for_nonneg_expansion`\n271\t   (consumes 6g.2's `realLineSpacetime_no_trappedSurface_*`;\n272\t   demonstrates framework non-degeneracy)\n273\t\n274\t§5 — Cross-module re-export:\n275\t4. `hawkingPenrose_focusingTime_pos` (audit P6 cross-bridge: same\n276\t   focusing-time bound as Penrose; re-exports `focusingTime_pos`\n277\t   from 6g.2 as substantive named API)\n278\t\n279\t§6 — Module marker.\n280\t\n281\t**First formalization in any proof assistant** of the Hawking-Penrose\n282\thypothesis bundle, the SEC-based correctness-push biconditional, and\n283\tthe cosmological-Λ ⟹ no-cosmological-singularity verification at the\n284\tabstract-relation level.\n285\t\n286\t**Curve-theoretic gap (same as 6g.2):** the curve-theoretic step\n287\t\"focal point ⟹ geodesic incompleteness\" requires Lorentzian-metric\n288\t+ timelike-geodesic infrastructure not yet in our Lean ecosystem.\n289\tThe abstract-relation form ships here.\n290\t\n291\t**Stages 10/11/13 deferred per user policy** (Mathlib-PR-style\n292\tinfrastructure; content lifts as D3 §25 per `PAPER_DRAFT_MAPPING.md`\n293\tPhase 6g addendum).\n294\t-/\n295\ttheorem _phase6g_w3_module_summary_marker : True := trivial\n296\t\n297\tend SKEFTHawking.HawkingPenroseSingularity\n298\t"}]},"uuid":"ad148973-58ac-48b5-8272-c5e6b71a9683","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:40:00.371Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5d7e9cdf-a40d-4884-8e69-1954e13e626e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}
{"parentUuid":"ed815d7b-df61-4556-bc4c-e297d43e45f3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a1b16a56a1a83bc81","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Gz8TWFLhM7fyJcvTw46gv2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport SKEFTHawking.Basic\n2\timport SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions\n3\timport SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure\n4\timport SKEFTHawking.PenroseSingularity\n5\timport SKEFTHawking.ExactSolutions\n6\timport Mathlib\n7\t\n8\t/-!\n9\t# Phase 6g Wave 4 — Hawking Area Theorem\n10\t\n11\t## Overview\n12\t\n13\tPhase 6g Wave 4. Hawking 1971: under NEC + cosmic censorship, the\n14\tarea of a black hole's event horizon is non-decreasing in time\n15\t(`dA/dt ≥ 0`). This is the **classical** version of the BH 2nd law,\n16\tdistinguishing it from the **thermodynamic** version (Phase 6a Wave 5\n17\t`BHThermodynamicsFourLaws.lean`, which derives `dS_BH/dt ≥ 0` from\n18\tSK-EFT entropy-current monotonicity in the substrate sector).\n19\t\n20\tThe area theorem is consistent with the thermodynamic 2nd law via\n21\tBekenstein-Hawking `S_BH = A/(4 G_N)`: a non-decreasing `A` directly\n22\timplies a non-decreasing `S_BH`. The two perspectives — geometric\n23\t(area theorem) and thermodynamic (Glorioso-Liu) — provide\n24\tindependent proofs of the BH 2nd law that converge in the SK-EFT\n25\temergent-gravity framework.\n26\t\n27\t## Scoping mode\n28\t\n29\tSame algebraic / abstract-relation level as 6g.2 + 6g.3. The\n30\tload-bearing content for the Schwarzschild specialization is the\n31\t**monotonicity of `M ↦ 16π M²` on `[0, ∞)`** — pure real algebra,\n32\tsubstantive but tractable.\n33\t\n34\tThe cosmic-censorship hypothesis is encoded as an abstract\n35\t`IsCosmicCensored` Prop on the spacetime; we ship the area\n36\ttheorem as `IsCosmicCensored ∧ NEC ⟹ AreaIsNonDecreasing`. The\n37\tdischarge of this for SCHWARZSCHILD (under classical mass\n38\taccretion only) is concrete.\n39\t\n40\t## Anti-pattern audit (per project preemptive-strengthening discipline)\n41\t\n42\t1. **No P1 ∃-absorption:** monotonicity is stated for explicit\n43\t   Schwarzschild masses with `M₁ ≤ M₂` hypothesis, not via opaque\n44\t   existential.\n45\t2. **No P2 bundle redundancy:** `IsHorizonAreaNonDecreasing` is a\n46\t   single-conjunct predicate (not bundled redundantly with NEC).\n47\t3. **No P3 trivial-mult-as-physics:** `schwarzschild_area_monotone`\n48\t   exercises a substantive `mul_le_mul` argument, not pure rfl-rename.\n49\t4. **No P4 vacuous axioms:** `IsCosmicCensored` is encoded\n50\t   abstractly; the area theorem's hypothesis bundle includes it as a\n51\t   substantive predicate (not auto-discharged).\n52\t5. **No P5 falsifier-restating-hypothesis:** the\n53\t   `area_theorem_classical_matches_6a5_thermodynamic` cross-bridge\n54\t   discharges via a `S_BH = A/4` algebraic identity — substantive,\n55\t   not a tautological restatement.\n56\t6. **Cross-module bridge integrity:** body imports\n57\t   `SKEFTHawking.ExactSolutions` and *calls* `schwarzschildArea`,\n58\t   `schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq`. Body imports\n59\t   `SKEFTHawking.PenroseSingularity` for the Riccati focusing core.\n60\t\n61\t## References\n62\t\n63\t- S.W. Hawking, *Phys. Rev. Lett.* **26**, 1344 (1971) (original\n64\t  area theorem paper).\n65\t- J.M. Bardeen, B. Carter, S.W. Hawking, *Commun. Math. Phys.* **31**,\n66\t  161 (1973) (BCH four laws; area theorem as the \"second law\"\n67\t  geometric version).\n68\t- R.M. Wald, *General Relativity* (1984), §12.2 (area theorem\n69\t  derivation).\n70\t\n71\t## Cross-module landscape\n72\t\n73\tThis module is consumed by:\n74\t- **6g.6 NoHairTheorem.lean** — uses area arguments for the BH-\n75\t  uniqueness proof\n76\t- Phase 6a Wave 5 `BHThermodynamicsFourLaws` — area theorem as the\n77\t  geometric counterpart of the thermodynamic 2nd law\n78\t-/\n79\t\n80\t@[expose] public section\n81\t\n82\tnamespace SKEFTHawking.AreaTheorem\n83\t\n84\topen SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions\n85\topen SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure\n86\topen SKEFTHawking.PenroseSingularity\n87\topen SKEFTHawking.ExactSolutions\n88\topen Set Real\n89\t\n90\t/-! ## §1 — Schwarzschild area monotonicity\n91\t\n92\tThe area of a Schwarzschild horizon is `A(M) = 16π M²`. This is\n93\tstrictly increasing in `M` for `M ≥ 0`. The classical area theorem\n94\ton Schwarzschild reduces to: under classical evolution (mass\n95\taccretion only, no Hawking radiation), `M(t)` is non-decreasing,\n96\thence so is `A(M(t))`.\n97\t-/\n98\t\n99\t/--\n100\t**Schwarzschild area is monotonically non-decreasing in mass:**\n101\tfor `0 ≤ M₁ ≤ M₂`, we have `schwarzschildArea M₁ ≤ schwarzschildArea M₂`.\n102\t\n103\tSubstantive: combines `schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq` (closed form\n104\tfrom 6f.4) with `mul_le_mul` on `M² ≤ M'²` for `0 ≤ M ≤ M'`. Load-\n105\tbearing for the area-theorem application to evolving Schwarzschild\n106\tblack holes.\n107\t-/\n108\ttheorem schwarzschild_area_monotone {M₁ M₂ : ℝ} (h₀ : 0 ≤ M₁)\n109\t    (h_le : M₁ ≤ M₂) :\n110\t    schwarzschildArea M₁ ≤ schwarzschildArea M₂ := by\n111\t  rw [schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq, schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq]\n112\t  have h_pi_nonneg : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 16 * Real.pi := by\n113\t    have : (0 : ℝ) ≤ Real.pi := Real.pi_nonneg\n114\t    linarith\n115\t  have h_M_sq_le : M₁ ^ 2 ≤ M₂ ^ 2 := by\n116\t    have h_M₂_nonneg : (0 : ℝ) ≤ M₂ := le_trans h₀ h_le\n117\t    nlinarith\n118\t  exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h_M_sq_le h_pi_nonneg\n119\t\n120\t/--\n121\t**Schwarzschild area is strictly increasing in mass for positive masses:**\n122\tfor `0 < M₁ < M₂`, we have `schwarzschildArea M₁ < schwarzschildArea M₂`.\n123\t\n124\tSubstantive: strict version of `schwarzschild_area_monotone`,\n125\tload-bearing for \"Hawking area theorem strict form\" on Schwarzschild\n126\tunder classical mass accretion. Uses `pow_lt_pow_left` for strict\n127\tmonotonicity of squaring on `(0, ∞)`.\n128\t-/\n129\ttheorem schwarzschild_area_strictly_increasing {M₁ M₂ : ℝ}\n130\t    (h₁_pos : 0 < M₁) (h_lt : M₁ < M₂) :\n131\t    schwarzschildArea M₁ < schwarzschildArea M₂ := by\n132\t  rw [schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq, schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq]\n133\t  have h_pi_pos : (0 : ℝ) < 16 * Real.pi := by\n134\t    have : (0 : ℝ) < Real.pi := Real.pi_pos\n135\t    linarith\n136\t  have h_M_sq_lt : M₁ ^ 2 < M₂ ^ 2 := by\n137\t    have h₂_pos : 0 < M₂ := lt_trans h₁_pos h_lt\n138\t    nlinarith\n139\t  exact mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left h_M_sq_lt h_pi_pos\n140\t\n141\t/-! ## §2 — Abstract area-monotonicity predicate\n142\t\n143\tEncodes the area-theorem hypothesis bundle abstractly: a function\n144\tof time (`area : ℝ → ℝ`) representing the BH event-horizon area is\n145\tnon-decreasing.\n146\t-/\n147\t\n148\t/--\n149\t**`IsHorizonAreaNonDecreasing area`:** the area function on the\n150\tevent horizon is monotonically non-decreasing in time, encoded as\n151\tthe predicate `Monotone area` on `area : ℝ → ℝ`.\n152\t\n153\tThis is the **statement** of the Hawking area theorem: under\n154\tNEC + cosmic censorship, the area function emerges as monotone.\n155\tAt the abstract level, we encode it as a predicate to be discharged\n156\ton concrete instances (Schwarzschild under classical mass accretion).\n157\t-/\n158\tdef IsHorizonAreaNonDecreasing (area : ℝ → ℝ) : Prop :=\n159\t  Monotone area\n160\t\n161\t/--\n162\t**Schwarzschild horizon-area is non-decreasing under monotone mass\n163\tevolution:** if `M : ℝ → ℝ` is non-decreasing (classical mass\n164\taccretion, no Hawking radiation) and `M(t) ≥ 0` for all `t`, then\n165\tthe horizon-area function `t ↦ schwarzschildArea (M t)` is also\n166\tnon-decreasing.\n167\t\n168\tSubstantive: composes `schwarzschild_area_monotone` with the\n169\tmonotone-mass hypothesis. Load-bearing instantiation of the Hawking\n170\tarea theorem on Schwarzschild under classical evolution.\n171\t-/\n172\ttheorem schwarzschild_horizon_area_nonDecreasing\n173\t    (M : ℝ → ℝ) (h_M_nonneg : ∀ t, 0 ≤ M t) (h_M_mono : Monotone M) :\n174\t    IsHorizonAreaNonDecreasing (fun t => schwarzschildArea (M t)) := by\n175\t  intro t₁ t₂ h_le\n176\t  exact schwarzschild_area_monotone (h_M_nonneg t₁) (h_M_mono h_le)\n177\t\n178\t/-! ## §3 — Cross-bridge to BCH 2nd law (6a.5)\n179\t\n180\tThe classical area theorem (6g.4) and the thermodynamic 2nd law\n181\t(Phase 6a Wave 5 `BHThermodynamicsFourLaws.lean`) are independent\n182\tproofs of BH entropy non-decrease. Their consistency is the\n183\tBekenstein-Hawking identity `S_BH = A / (4 G_N)`.\n184\t-/\n185\t\n186\t/--\n187\t**`IsBHEntropyNonDecreasing entropy`:** the BH entropy function\n188\t`entropy : ℝ → ℝ` is non-decreasing in time. This is the\n189\t*thermodynamic* statement of the BH 2nd law; via `S = A/(4G_N)`,\n190\tit is equivalent to the geometric `IsHorizonAreaNonDecreasing`.\n191\t-/\n192\tdef IsBHEntropyNonDecreasing (entropy : ℝ → ℝ) : Prop :=\n193\t  Monotone entropy\n194\t\n195\t/--\n196\t**Geometric area theorem implies thermodynamic 2nd law (G_N = 1):**\n197\tif the horizon area `area : ℝ → ℝ` is non-decreasing, then the BH\n198\tentropy `S(t) := area(t) / 4` (using natural units with `G_N = 1`)\n199\tis also non-decreasing.\n200\t\n201\tSubstantive cross-bridge: the Bekenstein-Hawking factor `1/4` is\n202\ta positive scaling; monotonicity transfers under positive scaling.\n203\tThis is the *geometric ⟹ thermodynamic* direction of the area-vs-\n204\tentropy bridge.\n205\t-/\n206\ttheorem area_theorem_implies_bh_entropy_monotone\n207\t    (area : ℝ → ℝ) (h_area : IsHorizonAreaNonDecreasing area) :\n208\t    IsBHEntropyNonDecreasing (fun t => area t / 4) := by\n209\t  intro t₁ t₂ h_le\n210\t  have : area t₁ ≤ area t₂ := h_area h_le\n211\t  linarith\n212\t\n213\t/-! ## §4 — Penrose-focusing connection\n214\t\n215\tThe classical area theorem proof uses the same Riccati focusing\n216\tinequality as Penrose's singularity theorem (6g.2). The horizon's\n217\tnull generators have non-negative expansion under NEC (else they\n218\twould focus and violate cosmic censorship); this reverses the sign\n219\tof the Riccati argument.\n220\t\n221\tWe expose the focusing-time-bound as a re-exported named consumer\n222\tto confirm the cross-module substrate connection.\n223\t-/\n224\t\n225\t/--\n226\t**Area-theorem focusing-time bound** (re-export of 6g.2):\n227\tthe focal-time bound `-3/θ₀` is positive when the initial expansion\n228\tis negative. Used in the area-theorem proof to argue that horizon\n229\tnull generators with `θ < 0` would form caustics within finite affine\n230\tparameter — but caustics on the horizon contradict cosmic censorship,\n231\thence horizon null generators must have `θ ≥ 0`, hence area is\n232\tnon-decreasing.\n233\t\n234\tSubstantive named alias of `focusingTime_pos` that documents the\n235\tsubstrate connection between area theorem and Penrose's argument.\n236\t-/\n237\ttheorem areaTheorem_focusingTime_pos (θ₀ : ℝ) (hθ₀ : θ₀ < 0) :\n238\t    0 < -3 / θ₀ :=\n239\t  focusingTime_pos θ₀ hθ₀\n240\t\n241\t/-! ## §5 — Module summary marker\n242\t\n243\tPhase 6g Wave 4 — Hawking Area Theorem.\n244\t\n245\t**Substantive theorems shipped (5 + 1 marker = 6):**\n246\t\n247\t§1 — Schwarzschild area monotonicity:\n248\t1. `schwarzschild_area_monotone` (substantive: `M ↦ 16π M²` is\n249\t   non-decreasing on `[0, ∞)`; uses `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left` +\n250\t   `nlinarith` on `M²` monotonicity)\n251\t2. `schwarzschild_area_strictly_increasing` (strict version on\n252\t   positive masses; uses `mul_lt_mul_left` + `nlinarith`)\n253\t\n254\t§2 — Abstract area-monotonicity:\n255\t3. `schwarzschild_horizon_area_nonDecreasing` (substantive\n256\t   composition: monotone mass + non-negativity ⟹ monotone area)\n257\t\n258\t§3 — Cross-bridge to BCH 2nd law:\n259\t4. `area_theorem_implies_bh_entropy_monotone` (substantive: geometric\n260\t   ⟹ thermodynamic via `S = A/4` Bekenstein-Hawking factor)\n261\t\n262\t§4 — Penrose-focusing connection:\n263\t5. `areaTheorem_focusingTime_pos` (re-export of 6g.2's focusing-time\n264\t   bound; documents the Riccati substrate connection)\n265\t\n266\t§5 — Module marker.\n267\t\n268\t**First formalization in any proof assistant** of:\n269\t- Schwarzschild area monotonicity in mass (substantive algebraic\n270\t  content)\n271\t- The geometric ⟹ thermodynamic BH 2nd law cross-bridge via\n272\t  Bekenstein-Hawking\n273\t- The Penrose-area-theorem substrate connection through the Riccati\n274\t  focusing inequality\n275\t\n276\t**Curve-theoretic gap:** the full area-theorem proof requires\n277\tshowing that horizon null generators have non-negative expansion\n278\tunder NEC + cosmic censorship — this is the curve-theoretic\n279\tcontent (Wald §12.2), deferred until Lorentzian-metric infrastructure\n280\tlands. We ship the **algebraic monotonicity content + the\n281\tpredicate-level area-theorem statement**, which is sufficient for\n282\tdownstream consumers (6g.6 NoHairTheorem).\n283\t\n284\t**Stages 10/11/13 deferred per user policy** (Mathlib-PR-style\n285\tinfrastructure; content lifts as D3 §26 per `PAPER_DRAFT_MAPPING.md`\n286\tPhase 6g addendum).\n287\t-/\n288\ttheorem _phase6g_w4_module_summary_marker : True := trivial\n289\t\n290\tend SKEFTHawking.AreaTheorem\n291\t"}]},"uuid":"44a188fa-c657-4100-9fd9-12a7b35b2d8d","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:40:00.371Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ed815d7b-df61-4556-bc4c-e297d43e45f3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}

{"parentUuid":"cc39fc38-03db-4f41-b1e9-536e20511a84","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a1b16a56a1a83bc81","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013PgpptAovvJL3ywfgWuFjg","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport SKEFTHawking.Basic\n2\timport SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure\n3\timport SKEFTHawking.ExactSolutions\n4\timport SKEFTHawking.AreaTheorem\n5\timport SKEFTHawking.KerrSchild\n6\timport Mathlib\n7\t\n8\t/-!\n9\t# Phase 6g Wave 6 — No-Hair Theorem (Vacuum Stationary Axisymmetric)\n10\t\n11\t## Overview\n12\t\n13\tPhase 6g Wave 6. Mazur 1982 / Bunting 1983 / Israel 1967: any\n14\tstationary axisymmetric vacuum black hole solution to Einstein's\n15\tfield equations is a member of the Kerr family, parameterized by\n16\tmass `M` and angular momentum `J` with sub-extremality bound\n17\t`|J| ≤ M²` (in geometric units).\n18\t\n19\tThe \"no hair\" theorem refers to the absence of additional\n20\tparameters: a vacuum BH is uniquely determined by the externally\n21\tobservable quantities `(M, J)`. (Charged extensions add `Q`; we\n22\trestrict to vacuum here per roadmap O.5 default.)\n23\t\n24\tPer Phase 6f deep-research audit: no proof assistant has formalized\n25\tthe no-hair theorem. **First formalization in any proof assistant.**\n26\t\n27\t## Scoping mode\n28\t\n29\tSame algebraic / abstract-relation level as 6g.1-6g.4. The Kerr\n30\tfamily is parameterized by `(M, J)` with `0 < M` and `J² ≤ M⁴` (sub-\n31\textremality). We ship:\n32\t- `KerrFamilyParams` structure with sub-extremality invariant\n33\t- Schwarzschild = `J = 0` specialization\n34\t- Cross-bridge to 6f.4 `schwarzschildArea` for the `J = 0` case\n35\t- The no-hair predicate as an abstract Prop on a vacuum BH\n36\t\n37\tThe full curve-theoretic discharge (Mazur's elliptic-PDE rigidity\n38\targument, or the Bunting σ-model approach, or Israel's\n39\tblack-hole-uniqueness theorem) requires Lorentzian-metric +\n40\tBel-Robinson energy + elliptic-regularity infrastructure not yet in\n41\tMathlib. We ship the abstract-predicate form here.\n42\t\n43\t## Anti-pattern audit (per project preemptive-strengthening discipline)\n44\t\n45\t1. **No P1 ∃-absorption:** the Kerr family is parameterized\n46\t   explicitly by `(M, J)`, not via opaque existential.\n47\t2. **No P2 bundle redundancy:** `KerrFamilyParams` bundles\n48\t   `(M, J, hM_pos, h_subext)` where each field is independently\n49\t   substantive.\n50\t3. **No P3 trivial-mult-as-physics:** `kerrFamily_extremality_implies_J_le_M_sq`\n51\t   exercises `nlinarith`-class square-root manipulation, not pure\n52\t   rfl-rename.\n53\t4. **No P4 vacuous axioms:** `IsKerrFamilyMember` is an explicit\n54\t   `Prop` matching `KerrFamilyParams`; not a placeholder.\n55\t5. **No P5 falsifier-restating-hypothesis:** the\n56\t   `schwarzschild_is_kerr_family_J_zero` theorem witnesses\n57\t   Schwarzschild as the `J = 0` specialization via concrete\n58\t   construction; not just unfolding.\n59\t6. **Cross-module bridge integrity:** body imports\n60\t   `SKEFTHawking.ExactSolutions` (for `schwarzschildHorizonRadius`),\n61\t   `SKEFTHawking.AreaTheorem` (for `schwarzschild_area_monotone`),\n62\t   and *calls* `schwarzschildHorizonRadius`,\n63\t   `schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq`.\n64\t\n65\t## References\n66\t\n67\t- W. Israel, *Phys. Rev.* **164**, 1776 (1967) (Schwarzschild\n68\t  uniqueness).\n69\t- B. Carter, *Phys. Rev. Lett.* **26**, 331 (1971) (axisymmetric\n70\t  uniqueness).\n71\t- D.C. Robinson, *Phys. Rev. Lett.* **34**, 905 (1975) (Kerr\n72\t  uniqueness).\n73\t- P.O. Mazur, *J. Phys. A* **15**, 3173 (1982) (σ-model proof).\n74\t- G.L. Bunting, PhD thesis, University of New England (1983) (σ-model\n75\t  refinement).\n76\t- M. Heusler, *Black Hole Uniqueness Theorems* (1996) (textbook\n77\t  treatment).\n78\t\n79\t## Cross-module landscape\n80\t\n81\tThis module is the final 6g wave (per roadmap §10). It consumes:\n82\t- 6f.4 `ExactSolutions` (Schwarzschild)\n83\t- 6g.1 `CausalStructure` (`Spacetime`)\n84\t- 6g.4 `AreaTheorem` (area monotonicity)\n85\t- Existing `KerrSchild.lean` (Kerr-Schild form)\n86\t\n87\tThere are no downstream 6g consumers; no-hair is the closing wave.\n88\t-/\n89\t\n90\t@[expose] public section\n91\t\n92\tnamespace SKEFTHawking.NoHairTheorem\n93\t\n94\topen SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure\n95\topen SKEFTHawking.ExactSolutions\n96\topen SKEFTHawking.AreaTheorem\n97\topen Set Real\n98\t\n99\t/-! ## §1 — Kerr-family parameters\n100\t\n101\tA Kerr black hole is parameterized by `(M, J)` with `M > 0` and\n102\tsub-extremality `|J| ≤ M²` (in geometric units, `c = G = 1`). At\n103\textremality `|J| = M²` the inner and outer horizons coincide; beyond\n104\textremality (`|J| > M²`) there is no horizon (naked singularity).\n105\t\n106\tWe require `J² ≤ M⁴` (the squared form of sub-extremality, more\n107\tconvenient for algebraic manipulations than the absolute-value\n108\tform).\n109\t-/\n110\t\n111\t/--\n112\t**`KerrFamilyParams`:** `(M, J)` parameterizing a Kerr family member,\n113\twith sub-extremality enforced as a structural invariant.\n114\t\n115\tFields:\n116\t- `M : ℝ` — ADM mass (geometric units)\n117\t- `J : ℝ` — angular momentum (signed; sign indicates spin direction)\n118\t- `hM_pos : 0 < M` — physical positivity\n119\t- `h_subext : J^2 ≤ M^4` — sub-extremality bound (Carter-Robinson)\n120\t\n121\tThe `J^2 ≤ M^4` form is equivalent to `|J| ≤ M^2` for `M > 0` and is\n122\tthe form most amenable to `nlinarith` proofs.\n123\t-/\n124\tstructure KerrFamilyParams where\n125\t  M : ℝ\n126\t  J : ℝ\n127\t  hM_pos : 0 < M\n128\t  h_subext : J ^ 2 ≤ M ^ 4\n129\t\n130\tnamespace KerrFamilyParams\n131\t\n132\tvariable (k : KerrFamilyParams)\n133\t\n134\t/-- The mass is non-negative (immediate from `0 < M`). -/\n135\ttheorem M_nonneg : 0 ≤ k.M := le_of_lt k.hM_pos\n136\t\n137\t/--\n138\t**Schwarzschild as the `J = 0` specialization of the Kerr family.**\n139\tA Schwarzschild BH with mass `M > 0` is a Kerr family member with\n140\tzero angular momentum. Substantive: explicit construction of the\n141\tsub-extremality witness `0 ≤ M^4` from `M > 0`.\n142\t-/\n143\tdef schwarzschildAsKerr {M : ℝ} (hM_pos : 0 < M) : KerrFamilyParams where\n144\t  M := M\n145\t  J := 0\n146\t  hM_pos := hM_pos\n147\t  h_subext := by\n148\t    have h_M_sq_nonneg : (0 : ℝ) ≤ M ^ 2 := sq_nonneg M\n149\t    have h_M_4 : M ^ 4 = (M ^ 2) ^ 2 := by ring\n150\t    rw [h_M_4]\n151\t    have : (0 : ℝ) ^ 2 = 0 := by norm_num\n152\t    rw [this]\n153\t    exact sq_nonneg (M ^ 2)\n154\t\n155\tend KerrFamilyParams\n156\t\n157\t/-! ## §2 — Sub-extremality consequences\n158\t\n159\tThe sub-extremality bound `J^2 ≤ M^4` has algebraic consequences:\n160\t`|J| ≤ M^2`, and for the Kerr horizon radius\n161\t`r_+ = M + √(M² - a²)` where `a = J/M`, we have a real positive root.\n162\t-/\n163\t\n164\t/--\n165\t**Sub-extremality implies `|J| ≤ M^2`.** The squared-form invariant\n166\t`J^2 ≤ M^4` is equivalent to `|J| ≤ M^2`.\n167\t\n168\tSubstantive: uses `abs_le_of_sq_le_sq'` (or square-root manipulation)\n169\tto convert squared form to absolute-value form. Real-analysis content\n170\ton the relationship between square and squared monotonicity.\n171\t-/\n172\ttheorem subextremality_implies_abs_J_le_M_sq (k : KerrFamilyParams) :\n173\t    |k.J| ≤ k.M ^ 2 := by\n174\t  have h_M_sq_nonneg : (0 : ℝ) ≤ k.M ^ 2 := sq_nonneg k.M\n175\t  -- Rewrite M^4 = (M^2)^2; squared sub-extremality becomes\n176\t  -- J^2 ≤ (M^2)^2 with M^2 ≥ 0, then abs_le_of_sq_le_sq' applies.\n177\t  have h_J_sq_le : k.J ^ 2 ≤ (k.M ^ 2) ^ 2 := by\n178\t    have h_M_4 : k.M ^ 4 = (k.M ^ 2) ^ 2 := by ring\n179\t    linarith [k.h_subext, h_M_4]\n180\t  -- abs_le_of_sq_le_sq' returns (-b ≤ a) ∧ (a ≤ b) given a² ≤ b² and 0 ≤ b\n181\t  have h_pair := abs_le_of_sq_le_sq' h_J_sq_le h_M_sq_nonneg\n182\t  exact abs_le.mpr h_pair\n183\t\n184\t/-! ## §3 — No-hair predicate (statement form)\n185\t\n186\tThe no-hair theorem at the abstract-predicate level: a stationary\n187\taxisymmetric vacuum BH spacetime contains a Kerr-family parameter\n188\twitness.\n189\t-/\n190\t\n191\t/--\n192\t**`IsStationaryAxisymmetricVacuumBH`:** abstract predicate\n193\tcharacterizing the no-hair theorem hypothesis. We DO NOT model the\n194\tunderlying Lorentzian-metric stationarity / axisymmetry / vacuum\n195\tfield equations explicitly at this layer (they require curve-\n196\ttheoretic + PDE infrastructure not yet in Mathlib); we ship the\n197\tpredicate as an abstract `Prop` to be discharged on concrete\n198\tinstances or via the eventual full Lorentzian-metric build-out.\n199\t\n200\tThe interpretation: `IsStationaryAxisymmetricVacuumBH S` holds iff\n201\tthe spacetime `S` is stationary (∃ timelike Killing vector),\n202\taxisymmetric (∃ spacelike rotational Killing vector), vacuum\n203\t(`Ric = 0`), and contains an event horizon.\n204\t-/\n205\tdef IsStationaryAxisymmetricVacuumBH (_S : Spacetime) : Prop := True\n206\t\n207\t/--\n208\t**`HasKerrFamilyDescription S`:** the spacetime `S` has a Kerr-family\n209\tdescription, i.e., there exist Kerr parameters `k : KerrFamilyParams`\n210\tfaithfully describing the BH content of `S`.\n211\t-/\n212\tdef HasKerrFamilyDescription (_S : Spacetime) : Prop :=\n213\t  ∃ k : KerrFamilyParams, k.M > 0\n214\t\n215\t/--\n216\t**No-hair theorem (statement form):** every stationary axisymmetric\n217\tvacuum BH spacetime has a Kerr-family description.\n218\t\n219\tAt the abstract layer, the LHS predicate is a placeholder (`True`)\n220\tbecause we do not model the load-bearing Lorentzian-metric\n221\tcontent. The substantive form of the theorem requires the curve-\n222\ttheoretic + PDE machinery of Mazur's σ-model proof and is deferred.\n223\t\n224\tWe ship the **statement** as a hypothesis-bundle bridge: the\n225\t`IsStationaryAxisymmetricVacuumBH` predicate, when discharged,\n226\tyields a Kerr-family description.\n227\t\n228\tPer the strengthening discipline, this theorem is **flagged as\n229\tMathlib-PR-infrastructure with curve-theoretic discharge deferred\n230\tto follow-on work**. We provide a substantive cross-bridge in §4\n231\t(Schwarzschild as Kerr-family member) instead, which IS discharged\n232\talgebraically from existing 6f.4 content.\n233\t-/\n234\ttheorem noHairTheorem_statement (S : Spacetime)\n235\t    (h_SAV : IsStationaryAxisymmetricVacuumBH S)\n236\t    (h_witness : ∃ M : ℝ, 0 < M) :\n237\t    HasKerrFamilyDescription S := by\n238\t  obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := h_witness\n239\t  exact ⟨KerrFamilyParams.schwarzschildAsKerr hM, hM⟩\n240\t\n241\t/-! ## §4 — Schwarzschild specialization (substantive cross-bridge)\n242\t\n243\tThe Schwarzschild solution (`J = 0`) is the simplest Kerr-family\n244\tmember. We provide substantive cross-bridges to 6f.4\n245\t`schwarzschildArea` and 6f.4 `schwarzschildHorizonRadius` to confirm\n246\tthat the Kerr-family abstraction reduces correctly in the `J = 0`\n247\tcase.\n248\t-/\n249\t\n250\t/--\n251\t**Schwarzschild is a Kerr-family member with zero angular momentum.**\n252\tFor any `M > 0`, the construction `schwarzschildAsKerr` yields a\n253\tvalid `KerrFamilyParams` with `M = M` and `J = 0`.\n254\t\n255\tSubstantive: the proof exercises `schwarzschildAsKerr`'s structural\n256\tfields and confirms field equality.\n257\t-/\n258\ttheorem schwarzschild_is_kerr_family_J_zero {M : ℝ} (hM_pos : 0 < M) :\n259\t    let k := KerrFamilyParams.schwarzschildAsKerr hM_pos\n260\t    k.M = M ∧ k.J = 0 := by\n261\t  exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩\n262\t\n263\t/--\n264\t**Schwarzschild horizon radius via Kerr parameters at `J = 0`:** the\n265\tSchwarzschild horizon radius `r_+ = 2M` (from 6f.4) matches the\n266\tKerr-family `J = 0` reduction. Substantive cross-bridge consuming\n267\t6f.4 `schwarzschildHorizonRadius`.\n268\t-/\n269\ttheorem kerr_family_schwarzschild_horizon_radius {M : ℝ} (hM_pos : 0 < M) :\n270\t    let k := KerrFamilyParams.schwarzschildAsKerr hM_pos\n271\t    schwarzschildHorizonRadius k.M = 2 * M := by\n272\t  show schwarzschildHorizonRadius M = 2 * M\n273\t  rfl\n274\t\n275\t/--\n276\t**Schwarzschild area via Kerr parameters at `J = 0`:** the\n277\tSchwarzschild horizon area `A = 16π M²` (from 6f.4) is the `J = 0`\n278\tspecialization of the Kerr family area formula. Substantive cross-\n279\tbridge consuming 6f.4 `schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq`.\n280\t-/\n281\ttheorem kerr_family_schwarzschild_area {M : ℝ} (hM_pos : 0 < M) :\n282\t    let k := KerrFamilyParams.schwarzschildAsKerr hM_pos\n283\t    schwarzschildArea k.M = 16 * Real.pi * M ^ 2 := by\n284\t  show schwarzschildArea M = 16 * Real.pi * M ^ 2\n285\t  exact schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq M\n286\t\n287\t/--\n288\t**Kerr-family Schwarzschild members satisfy the area-monotonicity\n289\ttheorem of 6g.4:** as the mass grows monotonically, the Schwarzschild\n290\tarea grows monotonically. Substantive cross-bridge to 6g.4\n291\t`schwarzschild_area_monotone`.\n292\t\n293\tThis is the no-hair connection to the area theorem: among Kerr\n294\tfamily members with `J = 0`, area is determined by mass alone, and\n295\tmass is the only parameter that can grow under classical accretion\n296\t(Hawking radiation excluded), hence area is monotone.\n297\t-/\n298\ttheorem kerr_family_schwarzschild_area_monotone\n299\t    {M₁ M₂ : ℝ} (hM₁_nonneg : 0 ≤ M₁) (h_le : M₁ ≤ M₂) :\n300\t    schwarzschildArea M₁ ≤ schwarzschildArea M₂ :=\n301\t  schwarzschild_area_monotone hM₁_nonneg h_le\n302\t\n303\t/-! ## §5 — Module summary marker\n304\t\n305\tPhase 6g Wave 6 — No-Hair Theorem (Vacuum Stationary Axisymmetric).\n306\t\n307\t**Substantive theorems shipped (7 + 1 marker = 8):**\n308\t\n309\t§1 — Kerr-family parameters:\n310\t1. `KerrFamilyParams.M_nonneg` (P3 trivial; documentation marker)\n311\t2. `KerrFamilyParams.schwarzschildAsKerr` (substantive structural\n312\t   construction: explicit witness for sub-extremality at `J = 0`,\n313\t   uses `sq_nonneg` non-trivially)\n314\t\n315\t§2 — Sub-extremality consequences:\n316\t3. `subextremality_implies_abs_J_le_M_sq` (substantive real-analysis:\n317\t   converts squared sub-extremality to absolute-value form via\n318\t   `abs_le_of_sq_le_sq'`)\n319\t\n320\t§3 — No-hair statement form:\n321\t4. `noHairTheorem_statement` (statement-level theorem with\n322\t   curve-theoretic discharge deferred; substantive use of the\n323\t   `schwarzschildAsKerr` constructor)\n324\t\n325\t§4 — Schwarzschild specialization:\n326\t5. `schwarzschild_is_kerr_family_J_zero` (field-equality cross-bridge)\n327\t6. `kerr_family_schwarzschild_horizon_radius` (substantive cross-\n328\t   bridge to 6f.4 `schwarzschildHorizonRadius`)\n329\t7. `kerr_family_schwarzschild_area` (substantive cross-bridge to 6f.4\n330\t   `schwarzschild_area_eq_16pi_M_sq`)\n331\t8. `kerr_family_schwarzschild_area_monotone` (substantive cross-\n332\t   bridge to 6g.4 `schwarzschild_area_monotone`; closes the no-hair\n333\t   ↔ area-theorem loop on Schwarzschild)\n334\t\n335\t§5 — Module marker.\n336\t\n337\t**First formalization in any proof assistant** of:\n338\t- The Kerr-family parameter structure with sub-extremality invariant\n339\t- The Schwarzschild = `J = 0` specialization as a substantive\n340\t  algebraic construction\n341\t- The no-hair-theorem-statement form bridging\n342\t  `IsStationaryAxisymmetricVacuumBH` to `HasKerrFamilyDescription`\n343\t\n344\t**Curve-theoretic gap:** the full Mazur σ-model rigidity argument /\n345\tBunting / Israel uniqueness require curve-theoretic + elliptic-PDE\n346\tinfrastructure not yet in Mathlib. We ship the abstract-predicate\n347\tform + the substantive Schwarzschild specialization here. Full\n348\tdischarge is deferred to follow-on work alongside Lorentzian-metric\n349\tinfrastructure.\n350\t\n351\t**Phase 6g closure milestone:** waves 6g.1, 6g.2, 6g.3, 6g.4, 6g.6\n352\tall SHIPPED at the abstract-relation / algebraic level. 6g.5\n353\tCauchyProblem deferred to structural-Prop scope. The 6g program is\n354\t**structurally complete** at our project's algebraic-precedent level.\n355\t\n356\t**Stages 10/11/13 deferred per user policy** (Mathlib-PR-style\n357\tinfrastructure; content lifts as D3 §27 per `PAPER_DRAFT_MAPPING.md`\n358\tPhase 6g addendum).\n359\t-/\n360\ttheorem _phase6g_w6_module_summary_marker : True := trivial\n361\t\n362\tend SKEFTHawking.NoHairTheorem\n363\t"}]},"uuid":"6ffadcc6-ca68-4304-ad07-eaddcddf4025","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:40:09.062Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cc39fc38-03db-4f41-b1e9-536e20511a84","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}

{"parentUuid":"2fcbddd4-9c10-46ae-91d6-47d697d1000f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a1b16a56a1a83bc81","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XSTxYY5VpKw92QEma9p5xN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport SKEFTHawking.Basic\n2\timport SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions\n3\timport Mathlib\n4\t\n5\t/-!\n6\t# Phase 6g Wave 1 — Causal Structure on Spacetimes\n7\t\n8\t## Overview\n9\t\n10\tPhase 6g Wave 1. Causal-structure infrastructure for the global / non-\n11\tperturbative GR program: causal future / past, chronological future /\n12\tpast, causality-condition hierarchy\n13\t(`Chronological ← Causal ← StronglyCausal ← StablyCausal ← GloballyHyperbolic`),\n14\tCauchy surfaces, and the Geroch-Bernal-Sánchez characterization\n15\t`GloballyHyperbolic ↔ ∃ Cauchy surface`.\n16\t\n17\tPer the Phase 6f deep-research audit (`Lit-Search/Phase-6f/Phase 6f\n18\taudit — Classical GR Lean infrastructure.md` §3E + §1A) and the\n19\t2026-04-29 catch-up audit (`memory:project_bonn_covariant_derivative_landed.md`):\n20\tno proof assistant has formalized causal structure on Lorentzian\n21\tspacetimes. Mathlib's pinned commit `8850ed93` reaches `IsRiemannianManifold`\n22\t(positive-definite only) and the Massot/Rothgang/Macbeth `CovariantDerivative`\n23\t+ `Torsion` (landed Apr 2026), but Lorentzian metrics, causal futures,\n24\ttrapped surfaces, and globally hyperbolic spacetimes are absent\n25\teverywhere. **First formalization in any proof assistant.**\n26\t\n27\t## Scoping mode (algebraic / abstract-relation, point-wise on metric)\n28\t\n29\tFollowing the 6f.1–6f.6 algebraic precedent, this module ships **at\n30\tthe abstract-relation level** rather than the differential-geometry-\n31\tof-curves level. A `Spacetime` is a topological space of events with\n32\ttwo abstract binary relations — `causal` (events causally connectable\n33\tby a future-directed causal curve, witnessing `q ∈ J⁺(p)`) and\n34\t`chronological` (timelike curve, `q ∈ I⁺(p)`) — satisfying the\n35\tWald §8.1 algebraic axioms (reflexivity, transitivity, push-of-causal-\n36\tinto-chronological). Specializations to Minkowski, de Sitter, and\n37\tSchwarzschild outside the horizon are concrete instances of this\n38\tabstract structure.\n39\t\n40\tThe differential-geometry-of-curves layer (curves as `ℝ → Event`\n41\twith future-directed-causal tangent at every point) requires\n42\tmanifold-level Lorentzian metric infrastructure that does not yet\n43\texist in Mathlib (`Bundle.RiemannianMetric` is hard-wired to\n44\t`InnerProductSpace ℝ` / positive-definite signature; the Bonn branch\n45\tthat landed `CovariantDerivative` did not extend to Lorentzian\n46\tsignature). The abstract-relation layer captures all the load-bearing\n47\tcontent for downstream 6g waves (6g.2 PenroseSingularity needs\n48\ttrapped-surface + global-hyperbolicity + NEC; 6g.4 AreaTheorem needs\n49\tevent-horizon-as-causal-boundary + Raychaudhuri; 6g.6 NoHairTheorem\n50\tis mostly Kerr-family-algebraic).\n51\t\n52\t## Anti-pattern audit (per project preemptive-strengthening discipline)\n53\t\n54\t1. **No P1 ∃-absorption:** all witness theorems use explicit\n55\t   constructions (Minkowski causal/chronological relations are given\n56\t   as concrete `Vec4`-level predicates, not `∃ R, IsCausal R`).\n57\t2. **No P2 bundle redundancy:** `IsGloballyHyperbolic` does not bundle\n58\t   `IsStronglyCausal ∧ IsCompactJplusJminus`; the latter conjunct is\n59\t   the substantive condition with Strongly-Causal a separately-named\n60\t   prerequisite. The chain implications are non-vacuous and proved\n61\t   per concrete instance.\n62\t3. **No P3 trivial-multiplication-as-physics:** the chain\n63\t   `Chronological ← Causal ← Strongly ← Stably ← Globally` is encoded\n64\t   so each layer adds a *substantive* condition (no tower of `.1`\n65\t   projections). For example, `IsStablyCausal` requires existence of a\n66\t   monotone time function — that's not just `IsCausal` plus a flag.\n67\t4. **No P4 vacuous axioms:** `Spacetime`'s axioms are derived from the\n68\t   abstract definitions: `causal_refl`, `causal_trans`,\n69\t   `chronological_subset_causal`, mixed-strict transitivity. Each is\n70\t   independently witnessed in Minkowski and independently load-bearing\n71\t   in downstream theorems.\n72\t5. **No P5 falsifier-restating-hypothesis:** the\n73\t   `minkowski_isGloballyHyperbolic` theorem witnesses the abstract\n74\t   `IsGloballyHyperbolic` predicate via concrete Minkowski causal\n75\t   structure; the proof exercises the Vec4-level forward-cone\n76\t   characterization, not just unfolds defs.\n77\t6. **Cross-module bridge integrity:** body imports\n78\t   `SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions` and reuses `Vec4`, `MetricTensor`,\n79\t   `IsNull`, `IsTimelike`, `IsFutureDirectedTimelike`,\n80\t   `minkowskiMetric`. The Minkowski causal-future characterization\n81\t   calls `minkowskiMetric` directly.\n82\t\n83\t## Naming conventions (Mathlib upstream-style)\n84\t\n85\t- Namespace: `SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure` (single-level).\n86\t- `Spacetime` is the name of the abstract structure (Wald, Hawking-Ellis\n87\t  convention; matches `Mathlib.Topology.Spacetime` if/when it lands).\n88\t- Causal/chronological future predicates: `causalFuture`/\n89\t  `chronologicalFuture`, returning `Set Event`.\n90\t- Causality conditions: `IsChronological`/`IsCausal`/`IsStronglyCausal`/\n91\t  `IsStablyCausal`/`IsGloballyHyperbolic` — `Is`-prefixed Prop on\n92\t  `Spacetime`.\n93\t- `IsCauchySurface S` predicate on `Set Event`.\n94\t\n95\t## References\n96\t\n97\t- R.M. Wald, *General Relativity* (1984), Chapter 8 (causal structure).\n98\t- S.W. Hawking, G.F.R. Ellis, *The Large Scale Structure of Space-Time*\n99\t  (1973), Chapter 6 (causality conditions, global hyperbolicity).\n100\t- R.P. Geroch, *J. Math. Phys.* **11**, 437 (1970) (globally hyperbolic\n101\t  ⇒ topology `ℝ × S` with `S` Cauchy surface).\n102\t- A.N. Bernal, M. Sánchez, *Commun. Math. Phys.* **243**, 461 (2003)\n103\t  (Geroch's theorem with smooth Cauchy surface; the converse direction).\n104\t- E. Minguzzi, M. Sánchez, *The causal hierarchy of spacetimes*,\n105\t  arXiv:gr-qc/0609119 (canonical modern review).\n106\t\n107\t## Cross-module landscape\n108\t\n109\tThis module is consumed by:\n110\t- **6g.2 PenroseSingularity.lean** — trapped-surface + globally-hyperbolic\n111\t  hypothesis input to Penrose's theorem.\n112\t- **6g.3 HawkingPenroseSingularity.lean** — same hypotheses, SEC variant.\n113\t- **6g.4 AreaTheorem.lean** — event-horizon definition uses J⁻ of\n114\t  future null infinity.\n115\t- **6g.5 CauchyProblem.lean** — Cauchy surface as initial-data slice.\n116\t- **6g.6 NoHairTheorem.lean** — stationary axisymmetric BH causal\n117\t  structure.\n118\t-/\n119\t\n120\t@[expose] public section\n121\t\n122\tnamespace SKEFTHawking.CausalStructure\n123\t\n124\topen SKEFTHawking.EnergyConditions\n125\topen Set\n126\t\n127\t/-! ## §1 — Spacetime structure\n128\t\n129\tA `Spacetime` packages a topological space of events with abstract\n130\tbinary relations encoding causal connectability. Following Wald §8.1,\n131\tthe load-bearing structural content is in the **algebraic axioms** on\n132\tthe relations: reflexivity, transitivity, chronological-into-causal,\n133\tand mixed-strict transitivity (`I⁺ ∘ J⁺ ⊆ I⁺` and `J⁺ ∘ I⁺ ⊆ I⁺`).\n134\t-/\n135\t\n136\t/--\n137\t**Spacetime as an abstract relational structure.** An event type with\n138\ta topology, a future-causal relation `causal`, and a future-chronological\n139\trelation `chronological`, satisfying the Wald §8.1 axioms.\n140\t\n141\tThe relations carry the physical interpretation:\n142\t- `causal p q ↔ q ∈ J⁺(p)`: there exists a future-directed causal curve\n143\t  from `p` to `q` (or `p = q`).\n144\t- `chronological p q ↔ q ∈ I⁺(p)`: there exists a future-directed\n145\t  timelike curve from `p` to `q`.\n146\t\n147\tWe DO NOT model the curves explicitly at this layer — the abstract\n148\trelations capture the same content modulo curve-existence axioms\n149\t(Hawking-Ellis Prop. 6.1.2 + 6.2.5).\n150\t-/\n151\tstructure Spacetime where\n152\t  /-- The set of events comprising the spacetime. -/\n153\t  Event : Type\n154\t  /-- Topology on the event set; needed for compactness conditions. -/\n155\t  topo : TopologicalSpace Event\n156\t  /-- The future-directed causal relation. `causal p q` reads as `q ∈ J⁺(p)`. -/\n157\t  causal : Event → Event → Prop\n158\t  /-- The future-directed chronological relation. `chronological p q` reads as\n159\t      `q ∈ I⁺(p)`. -/\n160\t  chronological : Event → Event → Prop\n161\t  /-- Causal relation is reflexive: `p ∈ J⁺(p)` via the constant curve at `p`. -/\n162\t  causal_refl : ∀ p, causal p p\n163\t  /-- Causal relation is transitive: concatenation of causal curves. -/\n164\t  causal_trans : ∀ {p q r}, causal p q → causal q r → causal p r\n165\t  /-- Chronological relation is transitive: concatenation of timelike curves. -/\n166\t  chronological_trans : ∀ {p q r},\n167\t    chronological p q → chronological q r → chronological p r\n168\t  /-- Chronological future is contained in causal future. -/\n169\t  chronological_subset_causal : ∀ {p q}, chronological p q → causal p q\n170\t  /-- Mixed-strict transitivity LEFT (Wald Prop. 8.1.2): a timelike curve from\n171\t      `p` to `q` followed by a causal curve from `q` to `r` can be perturbed\n172\t      into a timelike curve from `p` to `r`. -/\n173\t  chronological_causal_trans : ∀ {p q r},\n174\t    chronological p q → causal q r → chronological p r\n175\t  /-- Mixed-strict transitivity RIGHT (Wald Prop. 8.1.2 mirror): a causal curve\n176\t      from `p` to `q` followed by a timelike curve from `q` to `r` can be\n177\t      perturbed into a timelike curve from `p` to `r`. -/\n178\t  causal_chronological_trans : ∀ {p q r},\n179\t    causal p q → chronological q r → chronological p r\n180\t\n181\tattribute [instance] Spacetime.topo\n182\t\n183\tnamespace Spacetime\n184\t\n185\tvariable (S : Spacetime)\n186\t\n187\t/-! ## §2 — Causal/chronological futures and pasts as `Set Event`\n188\t\n189\tStandard set-builder notation for `J⁺`, `J⁻`, `I⁺`, `I⁻`. These are\n190\tprojections of the abstract relations into `Set Event`, useful for\n191\tstating compactness and Cauchy-surface conditions.\n192\t-/\n193\t\n194\t/-- The causal future `J⁺(p) := { q : causal p q }`. -/\n195\tdef causalFuture (p : S.Event) : Set S.Event := { q | S.causal p q }\n196\t\n197\t/-- The causal past `J⁻(p) := { q : causal q p }`. -/\n198\tdef causalPast (p : S.Event) : Set S.Event := { q | S.causal q p }\n199\t\n200\t/-- The chronological future `I⁺(p) := { q : chronological p q }`. -/\n201\tdef chronologicalFuture (p : S.Event) : Set S.Event := { q | S.chronological p q }\n202\t\n203\t/-- The chronological past `I⁻(p) := { q : chronological q p }`. -/\n204\tdef chronologicalPast (p : S.Event) : Set S.Event := { q | S.chronological q p }\n205\t\n206\t/- §2 NOTE — set-form helpers `mem_causalFuture_self`,\n207\t   `mem_causalFuture_trans`, `chronologicalFuture_subset_causalFuture`,\n208\t   `mem_chronologicalFuture_of_chronological_causal`,\n209\t   `mem_chronologicalFuture_of_causal_chronological` were CUT at\n210\t   first-pass strengthening: they would restate `causal_refl`,\n211\t   `causal_trans`, `chronological_subset_causal`,\n212\t   `chronological_causal_trans`, `causal_chronological_trans`\n213\t   (axioms of `Spacetime`) verbatim in `Set` form via projection,\n214\t   contributing zero new mathematical content (P3 trivial-discharge\n215\t   pattern). Downstream consumers should use the axiom-form fields\n216\t   directly via dot-notation (`S.causal_refl`, `S.causal_trans`, etc.)\n217\t   or rewrite into `Set` form locally with `unfold causalFuture` if\n218\t   needed. -/\n219\t\n220\t/-! ## §3 — Causality conditions\n221\t\n222\tThe chain `Chronological ← Causal ← StronglyCausal ← StablyCausal ←\n223\tGloballyHyperbolic`. Each layer is an additional substantive\n224\trestriction. We follow Wald §8.2 + Hawking-Ellis §6.4–6.6 conventions.\n225\t-/\n226\t\n227\t/--\n228\t**`IsChronological`:** the spacetime contains no closed timelike curves.\n229\tEquivalently, `chronological` is irreflexive (`¬ chronological p p`\n230\tfor all `p`). Wald §8.2 calls this *chronology condition*. -/\n231\tdef IsChronological : Prop := ∀ p : S.Event, ¬ S.chronological p p\n232\t\n233\t/--\n234\t**`IsCausal`:** the spacetime contains no closed *causal* curves through\n235\tany event other than the trivial constant curve. We encode this as: if\n236\t`q ∈ J⁺(p)` and `p ∈ J⁺(q)` then `q = p`. Wald §8.2 calls this\n237\t*causality condition*; it is strictly stronger than chronology. -/\n238\tdef IsCausal : Prop :=\n239\t  ∀ {p q : S.Event}, S.causal p q → S.causal q p → p = q\n240\t\n241\t/--\n242\t**`IsStronglyCausal`:** every event has arbitrarily small causally convex\n243\tneighborhoods. We encode this as: for every `p`, every neighborhood `U`\n244\tof `p`, there exists a sub-neighborhood `V ⊆ U` of `p` such that no\n245\tcausal pair `(q, r)` with `q ∈ V` and `r ∈ V` is connected by a causal\n246\tchain leaving `V` and returning. Wald §8.2 / Hawking-Ellis §6.6.\n247\t\n248\tFor our abstract-relation level, \"leaving V and returning\" is encoded\n249\tcombinatorially: there is no intermediate event `m ∉ V` with\n250\t`m ∈ J⁺(q)` and `r ∈ J⁺(m)`. -/\n251\tdef IsStronglyCausal : Prop :=\n252\t  S.IsCausal ∧\n253\t  ∀ p : S.Event, ∀ U ∈ nhds p, ∃ V ∈ nhds p, V ⊆ U ∧\n254\t    ∀ q ∈ V, ∀ r ∈ V, ∀ m : S.Event, S.causal q m → S.causal m r →\n255\t      m ∈ V\n256\t\n257\t/--\n258\t**`IsStablyCausal`:** the spacetime admits a continuous monotone time\n259\tfunction `t : Event → ℝ` strictly increasing along future-directed\n260\tchronological curves. Hawking-Ellis Prop. 6.4.9: this is equivalent to\n261\tthe existence of a slightly-wider-than-`g` Lorentzian metric on the\n262\tmanifold that is still causal — captured here by the time-function\n263\tformulation directly.\n264\t\n265\tThe strict monotonicity along *chronological* (not just causal) curves\n266\tis the load-bearing content; it implies `IsCausal` because a closed\n267\tcausal curve would violate strict monotonicity along its chronological\n268\tsub-segments. -/\n269\tdef IsStablyCausal : Prop :=\n270\t  ∃ t : S.Event → ℝ, Continuous t ∧\n271\t    ∀ {p q : S.Event}, S.chronological p q → t p < t q\n272\t\n273\t/--\n274\t**`IsGloballyHyperbolic`:** the spacetime is strongly causal and the\n275\t\"causal diamond\" `J⁺(p) ∩ J⁻(q)` is compact for every pair `(p, q)`.\n276\tThis is Wald Def. 8.3.1 / Hawking-Ellis §6.6.\n277\t\n278\tGeroch (1970) + Bernal-Sánchez (2003) established the equivalence\n279\t`IsGloballyHyperbolic ↔ ∃ Cauchy surface`. -/\n280\tdef IsGloballyHyperbolic : Prop :=\n281\t  S.IsStronglyCausal ∧\n282\t  ∀ p q : S.Event,\n283\t    IsCompact (S.causalFuture p ∩ S.causalPast q)\n284\t\n285\t/-! ## §4 — Cauchy surfaces -/\n286\t\n287\t/--\n288\t**`IsCauchySurface Sigma_set`:** every inextendible causal curve through\n289\tany event of the spacetime crosses `Sigma_set` exactly once. At the\n290\tabstract-relation level, we encode this as: every event lies in the\n291\tcausal future or past (or both) of some unique element of `Sigma_set`.\n292\tWald §8.3.\n293\t\n294\tThe \"exactly once\" condition is captured by the `ExistsUnique` form.\n295\tWe use `Sigma_set` rather than the conventional Σ since Σ is reserved\n296\tin Lean's syntax for sigma types. -/\n297\tdef IsCauchySurface (Sigma_set : Set S.Event) : Prop :=\n298\t  ∀ p : S.Event, ∃! sigma ∈ Sigma_set, S.causal sigma p ∨ S.causal p sigma\n299\t\n300\t/-! ## §5 — Causal hierarchy commentary\n301\t\n302\tThe textbook chain `Chronological ← Causal ← StronglyCausal ← StablyCausal\n303\t← GloballyHyperbolic` is non-trivial: each implication requires\n304\tgeometric content beyond the abstract axioms in §1.\n305\t\n306\tIn particular, **`IsCausal ⟹ IsChronological`** is NOT a consequence of\n307\tour six Wald §8.1 axioms alone. The textbook proof (Wald Prop. 8.2.1,\n308\tHawking-Ellis Prop. 6.2.6) uses curves-of-nonzero-length to convert a\n309\tclosed timelike loop into a non-trivial closed causal loop. Our\n310\tabstract relation layer cannot see \"non-zero length\", so this\n311\timplication needs an additional axiom (added on a per-instance basis\n312\tin §6 below for concrete spacetimes, where curve-theoretic content\n313\tdischarges it). We choose NOT to ship the abstract version as a\n314\ttheorem (it is not provable from the axioms in §1 — see the\n315\tpost-mortem in §7).\n316\t\n317\tLikewise, **`IsStablyCausal ⟹ IsCausal`** requires that a closed\n318\tcausal curve sit in the closure of closed timelike curves under small\n319\tmetric perturbations, which is curve-theoretic content. We ship the\n320\trelated substantive theorem `IsStablyCausal_implies_chronological` —\n321\ta strict-monotone time function rules out closed *timelike* curves\n322\tdirectly — as an honest substantive result here.\n323\t\n324\t**`IsGloballyHyperbolic ⟹ IsStronglyCausal`** is by-conjunction: the\n325\tformer is defined as `IsStronglyCausal ∧ ...`. We do not ship this as\n326\ta separate theorem (P3-trivial under our discipline). -/\n327\t\n328\t/--\n329\t**`IsStablyCausal ⟹ IsChronological`** — substantive: a continuous\n330\tstrictly-monotone-on-chronological time function rules out closed\n331\ttimelike curves. The argument: if `chronological p p`, then\n332\t`t p < t p` by strict monotonicity, contradiction. -/\n333\ttheorem IsStablyCausal_implies_IsChronological (h : S.IsStablyCausal) :\n334\t    S.IsChronological := by\n335\t  intro p hp\n336\t  obtain ⟨t, _, h_strict⟩ := h\n337\t  exact lt_irrefl (t p) (h_strict hp)\n338\t\n339\t/-! ## §6 — Concrete witness: real-line spacetime\n340\t\n341\tThe simplest non-vacuous Spacetime instance: events are real numbers,\n342\t`causal := (· ≤ ·)`, `chronological := (· < ·)`. This corresponds\n343\tphysically to a 1-dimensional time-only \"spacetime\" with no spatial\n344\tstructure — every causal curve is a monotone parametrization of `ℝ`.\n345\t\n346\tWe use this to demonstrate the framework is non-vacuous and to ship\n347\tsubstantive theorems exercising every causality condition. The\n348\tgeneralization to Minkowski / de Sitter / Schwarzschild (with full\n349\t4D causal structure) follows the same pattern with additional Vec4-\n350\tlevel algebraic content; we defer those to a follow-on subwave to\n351\tkeep this first ship tractable.\n352\t-/\n353\t\n354\t/-- The real-line spacetime: events are `ℝ`, with the standard order\n355\t    interpreted as causal/chronological future. Marked `@[reducible]`\n356\t    so structure-field projections (`Event = ℝ`, `causal = (· ≤ ·)`,\n357\t    `chronological = (· < ·)`) reduce in proofs. -/\n358\t@[reducible] def realLineSpacetime : Spacetime :=\n359\t  { Event := ℝ\n360\t    topo := inferInstance\n361\t    causal := (· ≤ ·)\n362\t    chronological := (· < ·)\n363\t    causal_refl := le_refl\n364\t    causal_trans := le_trans\n365\t    chronological_trans := lt_trans\n366\t    chronological_subset_causal := le_of_lt\n367\t    chronological_causal_trans := lt_of_lt_of_le\n368\t    causal_chronological_trans := lt_of_le_of_lt }\n369\t\n370\t/-- **Real-line spacetime is chronological:** `<` is irreflexive on `ℝ`,\n371\t    so no closed timelike curves exist. -/\n372\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_isChronological :\n373\t    realLineSpacetime.IsChronological := by\n374\t  intro p\n375\t  show ¬ (p < p)\n376\t  exact lt_irrefl p\n377\t\n378\t/-- **Real-line spacetime is causal:** `≤` is antisymmetric on `ℝ`,\n379\t    so a causal loop forces equality of the events. -/\n380\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_isCausal :\n381\t    realLineSpacetime.IsCausal := by\n382\t  intros p q hpq hqp\n383\t  exact le_antisymm (show (p : ℝ) ≤ q from hpq) (show (q : ℝ) ≤ p from hqp)\n384\t\n385\t/-- **Real-line spacetime is stably causal:** the identity function\n386\t    `t : ℝ → ℝ`, `t x = x`, is a continuous strictly-monotone time\n387\t    function for the real-line spacetime. -/\n388\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_isStablyCausal :\n389\t    realLineSpacetime.IsStablyCausal :=\n390\t  ⟨id, continuous_id, fun {p q} (h : p < q) => h⟩\n391\t\n392\t/-- **`IsStablyCausal_implies_IsChronological` instantiated on the\n393\t    real-line spacetime** — sanity check that the abstract chain\n394\t    closes on a concrete witness. The chain\n395\t    `IsStablyCausal ⟹ IsChronological` runs through the time-function\n396\t    irreflexivity argument from §5. -/\n397\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_chronological_via_stably :\n398\t    realLineSpacetime.IsChronological :=\n399\t  IsStablyCausal_implies_IsChronological _ realLineSpacetime_isStablyCausal\n400\t\n401\t/-- **Causal future on the real-line spacetime** is the upper-closed\n402\t    half-line: `J⁺(p) = [p, ∞)`. Substantive at the def-unfold + set-\n403\t    extensionality level. -/\n404\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_causalFuture_eq (p : ℝ) :\n405\t    realLineSpacetime.causalFuture p = Set.Ici p := by\n406\t  ext q\n407\t  show (p : ℝ) ≤ q ↔ q ∈ Set.Ici p\n408\t  rw [Set.mem_Ici]\n409\t\n410\t/-- **Chronological future on the real-line spacetime** is the strict\n411\t    upper half-line: `I⁺(p) = (p, ∞)`. -/\n412\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_chronologicalFuture_eq (p : ℝ) :\n413\t    realLineSpacetime.chronologicalFuture p = Set.Ioi p := by\n414\t  ext q\n415\t  show (p : ℝ) < q ↔ q ∈ Set.Ioi p\n416\t  rw [Set.mem_Ioi]\n417\t\n418\t/-- **Causal diamond on the real-line spacetime** is a closed bounded\n419\t    interval: `J⁺(p) ∩ J⁻(q) = [p, q]` (empty when `q < p`).\n420\t    Substantive content for the global-hyperbolicity verification:\n421\t    closed bounded intervals in `ℝ` are compact (Heine-Borel). -/\n422\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_causalDiamond_eq (p q : ℝ) :\n423\t    realLineSpacetime.causalFuture p ∩ realLineSpacetime.causalPast q =\n424\t      Set.Icc p q := by\n425\t  ext r\n426\t  show ((p : ℝ) ≤ r ∧ r ≤ q) ↔ r ∈ Set.Icc p q\n427\t  rw [Set.mem_Icc]\n428\t\n429\t/-- **Singleton at any time is a Cauchy surface in the real-line\n430\t    spacetime.** Substantive: every event `p ∈ ℝ` is connected to the\n431\t    unique `t₀` element by either `t₀ ≤ p` (causal future of `t₀`) or\n432\t    `p ≤ t₀` (causal past), with uniqueness from the singleton\n433\t    structure. -/\n434\ttheorem realLineSpacetime_singleton_isCauchySurface (t₀ : ℝ) :\n435\t    realLineSpacetime.IsCauchySurface ({t₀} : Set ℝ) := by\n436\t  intro p\n437\t  refine ⟨t₀, ?_, ?_⟩\n438\t  · -- existence: t₀ ∈ {t₀} and either t₀ ≤ p or p ≤ t₀\n439\t    refine ⟨rfl, ?_⟩\n440\t    by_cases h : t₀ ≤ p\n441\t    · exact Or.inl h\n442\t    · exact Or.inr (le_of_not_ge h)\n443\t  · -- uniqueness from the singleton structure\n444\t    intro y hy\n445\t    exact hy.1\n446\t\n447\t/-!
